FILE [1/92]: .github/workflows/approve.yml
================================================================================
name: Approve and upload (PASS)
on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      run_id:
        description: "Run ID of the Studio run (leave empty to pick latest)"
        required: false
        default: ""

permissions:
  contents: write
  actions: read

jobs:
  upload:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with: { python-version: "3.11" }
      - run: pip install google-api-python-client google-auth
      - name: Download video
        env: { GH_TOKEN: "${{ github.token }}" }
        run: |
          RUN_ID="${{ inputs.run_id }}"
          if [ -z "$RUN_ID" ]; then
            echo "No run_id specified. Finding latest Hourly Studio Reels run..."
            RUN_ID=$(gh run list --workflow="daily.yml" --status="success" --limit 1 --json databaseId --jq '.[0].databaseId')
          fi
          echo "Downloading artifact from run ID: $RUN_ID"
          gh run download "$RUN_ID" -n video -D data/output
      - name: Upload to YouTube
        env:
          YT_CLIENT_ID: ${{ secrets.YT_CLIENT_ID }}
          YT_CLIENT_SECRET: ${{ secrets.YT_CLIENT_SECRET }}
          YT_REFRESH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.YT_REFRESH_TOKEN }}
        run: python src/youtube_upload.py data/output/*/
      - name: Save completed book
        run: |
          git config user.name "studio-bot"
          git config user.email "bot@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add completed_books.md
          git diff --cached --quiet || (git commit -m "completed book" && git push)

================================================================================
FILE [2/92]: .github/workflows/daily.yml
================================================================================
name: Hourly Studio Reels
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: "0 * * * *"      # Every hour (at minute 0)
  workflow_dispatch:           # manual trigger button anytime

permissions:
  contents: write
  pages: write
  id-token: write

jobs:
  make-video:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 60
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with: { python-version: "3.11" }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: "20" }
      - run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y ffmpeg
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - run: npm install --no-audit --no-fund
        working-directory: remotion
      - run: npx remotion browser ensure
        working-directory: remotion
      - name: Make Reel Video
        env:
          LLM_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.LLM_API_KEY }}
          LLM_BASE_URL: ${{ secrets.LLM_BASE_URL }}
          LLM_MODEL: ${{ secrets.LLM_MODEL }}
          LLM_API_STYLE: ${{ secrets.LLM_API_STYLE }}
          TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN }}
          TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID: ${{ secrets.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID }}
          YT_CLIENT_ID: ${{ secrets.YT_CLIENT_ID }}
          YT_CLIENT_SECRET: ${{ secrets.YT_CLIENT_SECRET }}
          YT_REFRESH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.YT_REFRESH_TOKEN }}
        run: python src/daily_pipeline.py --headless
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: video
          path: data/output
          retention-days: 7
      - name: Build Mobile Review Website
        run: python src/review_app/build_pages_site.py ${{ github.run_id }}
      - name: Upload Pages Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with:
          path: public
      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4
        continue-on-error: true
      - name: Remember theme and template history
        run: |
          git config user.name "studio-bot"
          git config user.email "bot@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add data/theme_history.json data/template_history.json data/pending_video.json
          git diff --cached --quiet || (git commit -m "theme, template and pending video [skip ci]" && git push)

FILE [4/92]: config/settings.py
================================================================================
import os
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from dotenv import load_dotenv

# Load environment variables
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
load_dotenv(BASE_DIR / ".env")

@dataclass
class PathConfig:
    base_dir: Path = BASE_DIR
    config_dir: Path = BASE_DIR / "config"
    src_dir: Path = BASE_DIR / "src"
    data_dir: Path = BASE_DIR / "data"
    output_dir: Path = BASE_DIR / "data" / "output"

    def ensure_directories(self):
        for p in [self.config_dir, self.src_dir, self.data_dir, self.output_dir]:
            p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

@dataclass
class YouTubeSettings:
    client_secrets_file: str = os.getenv(
        "YOUTUBE_CLIENT_SECRETS_FILE", str(BASE_DIR / "config" / "client_secrets.json")
    )
    token_file: str = os.getenv(
        "YOUTUBE_TOKEN_FILE", str(BASE_DIR / "config" / "youtube_token.json")
    )
    default_privacy: str = os.getenv("DEFAULT_UPLOAD_PRIVACY", "private")
    scopes: list[str] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: [
            "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload",
            "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.readonly",
        ]
    )

@dataclass
class AppConfig:
    paths: PathConfig = field(default_factory=PathConfig)
    youtube: YouTubeSettings = field(default_factory=YouTubeSettings)

config = AppConfig()
config.paths.ensure_directories()

FILE [37/92]: migration_report.md
================================================================================
# 🚀 MindSpark Studio Migration Report

## 1. Executive Summary
The migration from the old project (`D:\youtube_automation`) to the new unified target project (`D:\mindspark`) is complete. All useful components, voice configurations, background music, authorized YouTube credentials, and mobile review capabilities from the old project have been merged with MindSpark's dynamic Remotion theme engine, variety rules, and Claude-powered script planner.

The combined project is tested and verified on Windows with 3 distinct end-to-end video generations.

---

## 2. Inventory of Changes

### What was Copied (Old Wins)
1. **`.env`**: Copied as-is to preserve all active API keys, Claude model/relay endpoints, and YouTube tokens. Mapped `ANTHROPIC_*` keys to `LLM_*` keys with `LLM_API_STYLE=openai`.
2. **`config/voice_config.json`**: Restored full voice configuration for **Voice 5 (Prabhat Neural, pitch -2Hz, rate +0%, locked)** with both old and new schema fields (`selected_voice_id`, `selected_voice_name`, `voice_model`, `voice`, `allow_wide`).
3. **`config/client_secrets.json` & `config/youtube_token.json`**: Restored local Google OAuth 2.0 app credentials and active channel refresh token for direct channel uploads.
4. **`completed_books.md`**: Restored complete markdown archive containing *Chanakya Neeti* so previously produced books are never repeated.
5. **`data/audio/bgm/ambient_lofi_pad.mp3`**: Restored studio background music (821 KB) used for automatic audio mixing ducked at -16 dB.
6. **`data/output/voices/`**: Restored voice audition clips (Madhur, Swara, Prabhat, Neerja) for the mobile review server's audio player.
7. **`data/output/samples/`**: Restored test samples and proof JSON files.
8. **`src/youtube/` & `scripts/`**: Copied YouTube Data API v3 upload engine, browser OAuth authorization scripts (`authorize_youtube.py`), and helper CLI tools.
9. **`connect_youtube.ps1` & `upload_video.ps1`**: Copied PowerShell shortcuts for channel connection and video publishing.
10. **`tests/test_youtube_upload.py`**: Copied test suite verifying configuration, authorization status, and dry-run execution.

### What was Replaced
1. **`.gitignore`**: Replaced to ensure secrets (`.env`, `config/client_secrets.json`, `config/youtube_token.json`, `*.mp4`, `node_modules/`, `data/work/`, `.venv/`) are strictly protected from source control.
2. **`requirements.txt`**: Combined modern dependencies from both projects (`edge-tts`, `requests`, `google-api-python-client`, `google-auth`, `google-auth-oauthlib`, `google-auth-httplib2`, `python-dotenv`, `pytest`).
3. **`src/review_app/mobile_server.py`**: Upgraded the basic review server to the complete 750+ line mobile PWA review application featuring dark mode UI, live video streaming, voice selector, BGM preview, and PASS / FAIL / EDIT buttons with local network IP detection for mobile phones.

### What was Skipped
1. **`xmrig-6.26.0-windows-x64.zip`**: External archive unrelated to video production.
2. **`remotion_explainer/node_modules` & `.venv`**: Generated environment folders (cleanly re-installed in MindSpark).
3. **Large intermediate video builds**: Intermediate raw test clips were omitted to keep the workspace clean.

---

## 3. Files Edited & Purpose

| File | Modification & Reason |
| :--- | :--- |
| **`src/common.py`** | Added fallback environment variable mappings so `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, and `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` automatically populate `LLM_API_KEY`, `LLM_BASE_URL`, and `LLM_MODEL`, setting `LLM_API_STYLE = "openai"`. |
| **`src/tts.py`** | Updated `_synth` to support both `cfg["voice"]` and `cfg["voice_model"]` so both voice configuration formats function interchangeably. |
| **`src/audio.py`** | Updated `mix()` to detect `data/audio/bgm/ambient_lofi_pad.mp3` and automatically mix it under the speech track at `-16 dB` with volume ducking and fade in/out, falling back to synthetic pad only if missing. |
| **`src/daily_pipeline.py`** | Fixed Windows command resolution (`npx.cmd` vs `npx`) and converted file paths to POSIX strings for `--props` arguments to prevent backslash parsing errors in Node.js. |
| **`src/youtube_upload.py`** | Unified upload logic to support both local OAuth credentials (`config/youtube_token.json`) and CI environment variables (`YT_REFRESH_TOKEN`), added markdown table logging to `completed_books.md`, and added dry-run safety. |
| **`tests/test_youtube_upload.py`** | Added root directory path resolution for pytest execution. |

---

Three consecutive videos were produced from different books. The MindSpark theme engine and history tracker (`data/theme_history.json`) ensured each video received completely distinct palettes, transitions, layouts, and sizes:

| Book | Palette | Accent Color | Transition | Layout | Pattern | Format Size |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |

| **Atomic Habits** | Graphite | `#FF6B6B` | `flip` | `center` | `grid` | **Vertical** (1080x1920) |
| **The Psychology of Money** | Neon Night | `#00F5A0` | `zoom` | `left` | `rays` | **Square** (1080x1080) |

- **Remotion Demo**: Verified (`remotion/out/demo.mp4`, 1.6 MB).
- **YouTube Test Suite**: 3/3 tests passed (`pytest tests/test_youtube_upload.py`).

## 5. Daily Production Commands

### Generate Daily Video
```powershell
# Pick an unread book automatically from library:
python src/daily_pipeline.py

# Or force a specific book:
python src/daily_pipeline.py --book "Deep Work"
```

### Start Mobile Review App
```powershell
python src/review_app/mobile_server.py
```
- Open on your PC: `http://localhost:8080`
- Open on your Phone (same Wi-Fi): `http://10.60.86.83:8080`

### Review Actions in Mobile App:
- **PASS**: Uploads to YouTube as private (or selected privacy), records book in `completed_books.md`, and cleans up temp files.
- **FAIL**: Discards and deletes the pending video.
- **EDIT**: Lets you adjust the YouTube title, description, and privacy directly before approving.

## 7. 2D Anime & Motion Comic (2.5D Parallax) Engine Upgrade

In response to the user's requirement for viral 2D Animation, Japanese Anime style, and Motion Comic (2.5D Parallax) video formats, the following components and pipeline features were built and verified:

### New Components (`remotion/src/components/anime/`):
1. **`AnimeCharacter.tsx`**:
   - Scalable 2D vector anime character with dynamic expressions (`focus`, `thinking`, `triumph`, `warning`).
   - Natural idle breathing via spring-based sine wave oscillation (`scaleY`).
   - Eye blinking system cycling every 80 frames.
   - Dynamic energy aura glow reacting to the current scene's theme accent color.
2. **`AnimeSpeedLines.tsx`**:
   - Procedural radial action speed lines with per-frame pseudo-random jitter for high-octane anime emphasis.
3. **`AnimeFloatingParticles.tsx`**:
   - Ambient floating bokeh/ember particle field drifting upwards with sinusoidal sway for multi-layer depth.
4. **`MangaPanels.tsx`**:
   - Split comic strip layout with manga screentone texture overlay, dynamic border glow, and comic badge spring tags.
5. **New Scene Types Registered in `SCENES` (`remotion/src/Video.tsx` & `src/llm.py`)**:
   - **`animeParallax`**: Multi-layer 2.5D parallax push-in where the background drifts, action speed lines fire, ambient particles float, and the anime character pans across with idle breathing.
   - **`motionComic`**: Split manga panels comparing "Problem vs Breakthrough" with reaction badges and side character animations.

### Verification:
- **Test Render**: `remotion/out/demo_anime.mp4` (7.8 MB, 735 frames, 1080x1920 vertical) rendered with zero errors.
- **Unit Test Suite**: 6/6 tests passing (`pytest tests/ -v`).
- **Backup Archive**: Verified baseline preserved at `d:\mindspark_backup_working_baseline.zip` and git tag/commits.

        ⬇️ Save Video
      </a>

  <script>
    const runId = "";
    const repo = "ITvALA/youtube_autometion";

    function showStatus(msg, isSuccess) {
      const box = document.getElementById("statusBox");
      box.style.display = "block";
      box.style.background = isSuccess ? "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)" : "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)";
      box.style.color = isSuccess ? "#34D399" : "#F87171";
      box.style.border = isSuccess ? "1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3)" : "1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)";
      box.innerHTML = msg;
    }

    async function approveAndUpload() {
      let token = localStorage.getItem("gh_token");
      if (!token) {
        token = prompt("Enter your GitHub Personal Access Token (with workflow permission) to trigger upload:");
        if (token) localStorage.setItem("gh_token", token);
        else return;
      }
      showStatus("⏳ Triggering YouTube upload workflow in GitHub Actions...", true);
      try {
        const resp = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/repos/${repo}/actions/workflows/approve.yml/dispatches`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + token,
            "Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json",
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({ ref: "main", inputs: { run_id: runId || "latest" } })
        });
        if (resp.ok) {
          showStatus("✅ SUCCESS! Upload triggered! Video will appear on YouTube channel in ~60 seconds.", true);
        } else {
          showStatus("⚠️ GitHub API error: " + resp.status + ". Token may lack workflow permissions.", false);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showStatus("⚠️ Request error: " + err.message, false);
      }
    }

    function rejectVideo() {
      if (confirm("Reject this video?")) {
        showStatus("❌ Video rejected. It will not be published.", false);
      }
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

FILE [40/92]: README.md

# MINDSPARK Studio (Remotion + Claude + GitHub Actions)

MindSpark is an automatic Hinglish book-summary YouTube Shorts / Reels studio.
Generates 1080x1920 vertical videos with 22 dynamic visual templates, automatic rotation, no-repeat memory, voiceover (edge-tts / elevenlabs), Remotion SVG/CSS animation, GitHub Actions cloud automation, and 1-click Telegram PASS/REJECT review.

---

## 🎨 22 Visual Video Templates (`config/templates.json`)
No two consecutive videos look the same! The pipeline automatically rotates across 22 distinct templates:

1. **`parallax_dawn`** — Parallax Dawn: warm mountain layers, rising sun, slow cinematic camera, big text.
2. **`neon_city_night`** — Neon City Night: glowing cyberpunk city skyline, illuminated windows, neon palette.
3. **`whisper_forest`** — Whisper Forest: serene pine forest silhouettes, floating fireflies, clean cards.
4. **`ocean_calm`** — Ocean Calm: layered ocean waves, horizon boat, sunset/teal palette.
5. **`starry_wisdom`** — Starry Wisdom: infinite night sky, glowing celestial moon, shooting stars.
6. **`spark_talk`** — Spark Talk: original mascot Spark narrates with animated speech bubbles.
7. **`spark_quiz`** — Spark Quiz: interactive test question with 3 options and animated reveal checkmark.
8. **`kinetic_burst`** — Kinetic Typography Burst: high-energy typography popping on the audio beat.
9. **`minimal_paper`** — Minimal Paper: light flat paper aesthetic, thin lines, clean cards, calm motion.
10. **`retro_terminal`** — Retro Terminal: monospace green/cyan console, typewriter text, blinking cursor, scanlines.
11. **`sticky_notes`** — Sticky Notes Board: colorful sticky notes pinned to a board with playful tilt/wobble.
12. **`comic_panels`** — Comic Panels: split manga/comic strip cells, halftone dots, bold action badges.
13. **`chalkboard_lesson`** — Chalkboard Lesson: vintage classroom chalkboard, chalk handwriting boxes and doodles.
14. **`timeline_journey`** — Timeline Journey: progression road with milestone nodes and traveling markers.
15. **`top_n_countdown`** — Top-N Countdown: big numbered ranking badges (`#3`, `#2`, `#1`) with sliding cards.
16. **`before_after`** — Before vs After: split screen comparison (old habit trap vs new protocol shift).
17. **`quote_poster`** — Quote Poster: editorial typographic poster with ornamental double border and author badge.
18. **`data_story`** — Data Story: compounding growth curves, radial dials, and metric spikes.
19. **`habit_tracker`** — Sunrise Habit Tracker: daily protocol checklist with animated checkmarks and streak badge.
20. **`cyber_glitch`** — Neon Glitch Cyber: futuristic digital grid with RGB chromatic aberration and data HUD.
21. **`paper_cut_layers`** — Paper-Cut Layers: multi-tier physical papercraft silhouettes with soft drop shadows.
22. **`gradient_flow`** — Gradient Flow: modern morphing gradient blobs behind sleek kinetic typography.

---

## ⚙️ How to Switch Template Modes (`config/style.json`)

Configure how templates are selected in `config/style.json`:

```json
{
  "template_mode": "random",
  "fixed_id": "parallax_dawn",
  "no_repeat_last": 7
}
```

* **`"template_mode": "random"`** *(Recommended)*:
  Weighted random pick across all enabled templates, strictly guaranteeing that **no template repeats within the last N videos** (default `no_repeat_last: 7`).
* **`"template_mode": "rotate"`**:
  Cycles through all 22 templates in sequential order based on history count.
* **`"template_mode": "fixed"`**:
  Always renders the specific template defined in `"fixed_id"` (e.g. `"retro_terminal"`).

---

## 📱 Strict 9:16 Vertical Video Rule (Hard Constraint)
* **Resolution**: All 22 templates and Remotion compositions are strictly locked to `1080x1920` (9:16 Shorts/Reels).
* **Safe Areas**: Key text is kept strictly inside the 9:16 safe zone (top 8% and bottom 14% clear of UI overlays).
* **ffprobe Verification**: Every rendered video is automatically checked with `ffprobe` prior to review or upload.

---

## 🚀 Running the Studio

### 1. Local Pipeline
```bash
# Generate video with automatic template selection
python src/daily_pipeline.py

# Force specific book or template
python src/daily_pipeline.py --book "Atomic Habits" --template "retro_terminal"

# Run tests
python -m pytest tests/
python src/test_templates.py
```

### 2. GitHub Actions (Cloud Automation)
* Workflows run automatically every hour (`Hourly Studio Reels`) in GitHub Actions.
* The generated 9:16 video is sent directly to your Telegram bot with **`[ 🚀 PASS & UPLOAD TO YOUTUBE ]`** and **`[ ❌ REJECT ]`** buttons.
* Tapping **PASS** immediately uploads the video to YouTube and replies with the live link!

FILE [42/92]: remotion/src/components/anime/AnimeCharacter.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame, interpolate, spring, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";

export type AnimeEmotion = "focus" | "thinking" | "triumph" | "warning";

export interface AnimeCharacterProps {
  emotion?: AnimeEmotion;
  accentColor?: string;
  u: number;
  scale?: number;
}

export const AnimeCharacter: React.FC<AnimeCharacterProps> = ({
  emotion = "focus",
  accentColor = "#00F5A0",
  u,
  scale = 1.0,
}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();

  // 1. 2D Idle Natural Dynamics
  const breathe = Math.sin(frame * 0.15) * 0.015;
  const hoverY = Math.sin(frame * 0.1) * (0.8 * u);

  // 2. Eye Blink calculation: blinks for 3 frames every 80 frames
  const blinkCycle = frame % 80;
  const isBlinking = blinkCycle > 74 && blinkCycle < 78;
  const eyeScaleY = isBlinking ? 0.1 : 1.0;

  // 3. Emotion-specific aura colors & highlights
  const auraColor =
    emotion === "focus"
      ? accentColor
      : emotion === "triumph"
      ? "#FFD700"
      : emotion === "warning"
      ? "#EF4444"
      : "#38BDF8";

  // Pop-in spring entrance
  const entrance = spring({
    frame,
    fps,
    config: {damping: 12, mass: 0.6},
  });

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "relative",
        width: 48 * u,
        height: 58 * u,
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        transform: `translateY(${hoverY}px) scale(${entrance * scale})`,
        transformOrigin: "bottom center",
      }}
    >
      {/* 🌟 ENERGETIC ANIME AURA GLOW */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          width: "90%",
          height: "90%",
          borderRadius: "50%",
          background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${auraColor} 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 70%)`,
          opacity: 0.35 + Math.sin(frame * 0.2) * 0.15,
          filter: "blur(24px)",
          zIndex: 0,
        }}
      />

      {/* 🥋 VECTOR ANIME CHARACTER FIGURE */}
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 200 240"
        style={{
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          overflow: "visible",
          zIndex: 1,
          transform: `scaleY(${1 + breathe})`,
          transformOrigin: "bottom center",
        }}
      >
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="hairGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#1E293B" />
            <stop offset="60%" stopColor="#0F172A" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor={auraColor} stopOpacity="0.8" />
          </linearGradient>
          <linearGradient id="skinGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FFE0BD" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#F6C89F" />
          </linearGradient>
          <linearGradient id="jacketGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#18181B" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#09090B" />
          </linearGradient>
          <filter id="animeGlow">
            <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="3" result="blur" />
            <feComposite in="SourceGraphic" in2="blur" operator="over" />
          </filter>
        </defs>

        {/* --- BODY / JACKET --- */}
        <path
          d="M 50 170 C 50 145, 150 145, 150 170 L 175 240 L 25 240 Z"
          fill="url(#jacketGrad)"
          stroke="#000"
          strokeWidth="3.5"
        />

        {/* High-tech collar & tie / core element */}
        <path
          d="M 85 152 L 100 175 L 115 152 Z"
          fill={auraColor}
          filter="url(#animeGlow)"
        />
        <path
          d="M 75 145 L 100 185 L 125 145"
          fill="none"
          stroke="#27272A"
          strokeWidth="3"
        />

        {/* --- NECK & JAW --- */}
        <path
          d="M 88 128 L 88 152 L 112 152 L 112 128 Z"
          fill="#EDB88B"
          stroke="#000"
          strokeWidth="2.5"
        />

        {/* --- HEAD / FACE (Sharp Anime Chin) --- */}
        <path
          d="M 65 80 C 65 118, 85 142, 100 146 C 115 142, 135 118, 135 80 C 135 48, 65 48, 65 80 Z"
          fill="url(#skinGrad)"
          stroke="#000"
          strokeWidth="3"
        />

        {/* Subtle Anime Cheek Blush / Highlight */}
        <ellipse cx="78" cy="112" rx="6" ry="2.5" fill="#F43F5E" opacity="0.4" />
        <ellipse cx="122" cy="112" rx="6" ry="2.5" fill="#F43F5E" opacity="0.4" />

        {/* --- NOSE --- */}

            strokeLinecap="round"

FILE [43/92]: remotion/src/components/anime/AnimeFloatingParticles.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, AbsoluteFill} from "remotion";

export interface AnimeParticlesProps {
  color?: string;
  count?: number;
  speed?: number;
}

export const AnimeFloatingParticles: React.FC<AnimeParticlesProps> = ({
  color = "#00F5A0",
  count = 24,
  speed = 1.2,
}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const {height} = useVideoConfig();

  const particles = Array.from({length: count}, (_, i) => {
    // Deterministic position based on index
    const seedX = (i * 37 + 13) % 100;
    const seedY = (i * 53 + 71) % 100;
    const size = 3 + ((i * 11) % 6);
    const zDepth = 0.5 + ((i * 7) % 10) * 0.1; // 0.5 to 1.4

    // Upward flow + gentle wave drift
    const yOffset = ((seedY - (frame * speed * zDepth * 0.4)) % 120 + 120) % 120 - 10;
    const xOffset = seedX + Math.sin(frame * 0.04 + i) * 3;
    const opacity = 0.2 + (Math.sin(frame * 0.08 + i * 2) + 1) * 0.25;

    return {
      x: xOffset,
      y: yOffset,
      size,
      opacity,
      key: i,
    };
  });

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{pointerEvents: "none", overflow: "hidden"}}>
      {particles.map((p) => (
        <div
          key={p.key}
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            left: `${p.x}%`,
            top: `${p.y}%`,
            width: `${p.size}px`,
            height: `${p.size}px`,
            borderRadius: "50%",
            backgroundColor: color,
            opacity: p.opacity,
            boxShadow: `0 0 ${p.size * 2}px ${color}`,
            transform: "translate(-50%, -50%)",
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

FILE [44/92]: remotion/src/components/anime/AnimeSpeedLines.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame, AbsoluteFill} from "remotion";

export interface AnimeSpeedLinesProps {
  color?: string;
  count?: number;
  opacity?: number;
  center?: [number, number]; // Percentage [x, y]
}

export const AnimeSpeedLines: React.FC<AnimeSpeedLinesProps> = ({
  color = "#FFFFFF",
  count = 36,
  opacity = 0.25,
  center = [50, 50],
}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const step = 360 / count;

  // Jitter seed changes every 2 frames for that authentic hand-drawn anime look
  const jitterSeed = Math.floor(frame / 2);

  const lines = Array.from({length: count}, (_, i) => {
    const angle = i * step + ((jitterSeed * 17 + i * 31) % 4) - 2;
    const rad = (angle * Math.PI) / 180;
    const innerRadius = 28 + ((jitterSeed * 7 + i * 13) % 12);
    const outerRadius = 140;

    const x1 = center[0] + Math.cos(rad) * innerRadius;
    const y1 = center[1] + Math.sin(rad) * innerRadius;
    const x2 = center[0] + Math.cos(rad) * outerRadius;
    const y2 = center[1] + Math.sin(rad) * outerRadius;
    const strokeWidth = 0.35 + ((i + jitterSeed) % 3) * 0.3;

    return {x1, y1, x2, y2, strokeWidth, key: i};
  });

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{pointerEvents: "none", overflow: "hidden", opacity}}>
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 100 100"
        preserveAspectRatio="none"
        style={{width: "100%", height: "100%"}}
      >
        {lines.map((line) => (
          <line
            key={line.key}
            x1={`${line.x1}%`}
            y1={`${line.y1}%`}
            x2={`${line.x2}%`}
            y2={`${line.y2}%`}
            stroke={color}
            strokeWidth={line.strokeWidth}
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        ))}
      </svg>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, spring, interpolate, AbsoluteFill} from "remotion";

export interface MangaPanelsProps {
  panel1Title: string;
  panel1Subtitle: string;
  panel2Title: string;
  panel2Subtitle: string;
  badgeText?: string;
  accentColor?: string;
  u: number;
}

export const MangaPanels: React.FC<MangaPanelsProps> = ({
  panel1Title,
  panel1Subtitle,
  panel2Title,
  panel2Subtitle,
  badgeText = "CRITICAL LESSON",
  accentColor = "#FF6B6B",
  u,
}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();

  // Entrance springs
  const popBadge = spring({frame: Math.max(0, frame - 5), fps, config: {damping: 9, mass: 0.5}});
  const slideLeft = spring({frame: Math.max(0, frame - 8), fps, config: {damping: 14}});
  const slideRight = spring({frame: Math.max(0, frame - 18), fps, config: {damping: 14}});

  // Camera subtle shift
  const camPan = interpolate(frame, [0, 90], [0, 8], {extrapolateRight: "clamp"});

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        width: "100%",
        display: "flex",
        flexDirection: "column",
        gap: 3 * u,
        transform: `translateY(${-camPan}px)`,
      }}
    >
      {/* 💥 COMIC IMPACT BADGE */}
      {badgeText && (
        <div style={{alignSelf: "center", transform: `scale(${popBadge})`}}>
          <span
            style={{
              background: accentColor,
              color: "#000",
              fontWeight: 900,
              fontSize: 3.8 * u,
              padding: `${0.8 * u}px ${2.4 * u}px`,
              borderRadius: 6,
              letterSpacing: 2,
              textTransform: "uppercase",
              boxShadow: `0 0 16px ${accentColor}`,
              border: "2px solid #000",
              display: "inline-block",
              transform: "rotate(-2deg)",
            }}
          >
            ⚡ {badgeText}
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 📖 PANEL 1: THE TRAP / PROBLEM */}

          border: "3px solid #27272A",
          borderLeft: `8px solid ${accentColor}`,
          borderRadius: 16,
          padding: `${2.8 * u}px ${4 * u}px`,
          transform: `translateX(${(1 - slideLeft) * -40}px)`,
          opacity: slideLeft,
          backdropFilter: "blur(12px)",
          boxShadow: "0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)",
        }}
      >
        <div style={{fontSize: 3.4 * u, color: "#A1A1AA", fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 1}}>
          01 • STATUS QUO
        </div>
        <div style={{fontSize: 6.2 * u, fontWeight: 900, color: "#FFF", lineHeight: 1.15, marginTop: u}}>
          {panel1Title}
        </div>
        <div style={{fontSize: 4.2 * u, color: accentColor, fontWeight: 700, marginTop: 0.8 * u}}>
          {panel1Subtitle}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 📖 PANEL 2: THE ANIME PROTOCOL / SOLUTION */}
      <div
        style={{
          background: "rgba(10, 25, 20, 0.9)",
          border: "3px solid #064E3B",
          borderRight: `8px solid #10B981`,
          borderRadius: 16,
          padding: `${2.8 * u}px ${4 * u}px`,
          transform: `translateX(${(1 - slideRight) * 40}px)`,
          opacity: slideRight,
          backdropFilter: "blur(12px)",
          boxShadow: "0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)",
        }}
      >
        <div style={{fontSize: 3.4 * u, color: "#6EE7B7", fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 1}}>
          02 • THE PROTOCOL
        </div>
        <div style={{fontSize: 6.2 * u, fontWeight: 900, color: "#FFF", lineHeight: 1.15, marginTop: u}}>
          {panel2Title}

        <div style={{fontSize: 4.2 * u, color: "#10B981", fontWeight: 700, marginTop: 0.8 * u}}>

FILE [46/92]: remotion/src/components/scenes/AnimeParallaxScene.tsx

import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, interpolate, spring, AbsoluteFill} from "remotion";
import {AnimeCharacter, AnimeEmotion} from "../anime/AnimeCharacter";
import {AnimeSpeedLines} from "../anime/AnimeSpeedLines";
import {AnimeFloatingParticles} from "../anime/AnimeFloatingParticles";

export interface AnimeParallaxSceneProps {
  s: any;
  plan: any;
  u: number;
}

export const AnimeParallaxScene: React.FC<AnimeParallaxSceneProps> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps, durationInFrames} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const emotion: AnimeEmotion = (d.emotion as AnimeEmotion) || "focus";
  const showSpeedLines = d.speedLines !== false;

  // 1. Cinematic 2.5D Parallax Camera: slow push-in + subtle tracking pan
  const camZoom = interpolate(frame, [0, durationInFrames], [1.0, 1.15], {
    extrapolateRight: "clamp",
  });
  const bgPanX = interpolate(frame, [0, durationInFrames], [-20, 20]);
  const charPanX = interpolate(frame, [0, durationInFrames], [15, -15]); // Opposing parallax depth

  // 2. Text entrance springs
  const badgePop = spring({frame: Math.max(0, frame - 4), fps, config: {damping: 10, mass: 0.6}});
  const titleSpring = spring({frame: Math.max(0, frame - 10), fps, config: {damping: 12}});

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill
      style={{
        overflow: "hidden",
        display: "flex",
        flexDirection: "column",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        padding: `${6 * u}px ${4 * u}px`,
        transform: `scale(${camZoom})`,
      }}
    >
      {/* --- LAYER 1: 2.5D PARALLAX BACKGROUND SPEED LINES & GLOW --- */}
      {showSpeedLines && (
        <AnimeSpeedLines
          color={c.ac}
          count={32}
          opacity={0.3}
          center={[50 + bgPanX * 0.1, 50]}
        />
      )}

      {/* Atmospheric embers / particles */}
      <AnimeFloatingParticles color={c.ac} count={20} speed={1.0} />

      {/* --- LAYER 2: TOP ANIME BADGE & HEADLINE --- */}
      <div
        style={{
          zIndex: 10,
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 1.5 * u,
          width: "100%",
        }}
      >
        {d.badge && (
          <div style={{transform: `scale(${badgePop})`}}>
            <span
              style={{
                background: c.ac,
                color: c.bg1,
                fontSize: 3.4 * u,
                fontWeight: 900,
                padding: `${0.6 * u}px ${2.2 * u}px`,
                borderRadius: 8,
                letterSpacing: 2,
                textTransform: "uppercase",
                boxShadow: `0 0 20px ${c.ac}`,
                display: "inline-block",
              }}
            >
              ⚡ {d.badge}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {d.title && (
          <div
            style={{
              fontSize: 8.5 * u,
              fontWeight: 900,
              lineHeight: 1.1,
              textAlign: "center",
              color: "#FFF",
              textShadow: `0 4px 18px rgba(0,0,0,0.8), 0 0 30px ${c.ac}40`,
              transform: `translateY(${(1 - titleSpring) * -20}px)`,
              opacity: titleSpring,
            }}
          >
            {d.title}
          </div>
        )}

        {d.subtitle && (
          <div
            style={{
              fontSize: 4.2 * u,
              fontWeight: 700,
              color: c.ac,
              opacity: titleSpring,
            }}
          >
            {d.subtitle}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* --- LAYER 3: 2D ANIME CHARACTER WITH PARALLAX OFFSET --- */}
      <div
        style={{
          zIndex: 5,
          transform: `translateX(${charPanX}px)`,
          transition: "transform 0.1s linear",
          marginTop: "auto",
        }}
      >
        <AnimeCharacter emotion={emotion} accentColor={c.ac} u={u} scale={1.12} />
      </div>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {Composition, CalculateMetadataFunction} from "remotion";
import {Main, Plan} from "./Video";
import demo from "../../data/demo_plan.json";

const FPS = 30;
const calc: CalculateMetadataFunction<Plan> = ({props}) => ({
  durationInFrames: Math.max(30, props.scenes.reduce((a, s) => a + Math.round(s.durationSec * FPS), 0)),
  width: 1080,
  height: 1920,
  fps: FPS,
});

export const Root: React.FC = () => (
  <Composition
    id="Main"
    component={Main}
    durationInFrames={300}
    fps={FPS}
    width={1080}
    height={1920}
    defaultProps={demo as unknown as Plan}
    calculateMetadata={calc}
  />
);

================================================================================
FILE [50/92]: remotion/src/Story.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import {AbsoluteFill, Easing, interpolate, spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";
import type {Plan, Scene} from "./Video";

/*
 * Two automatic scene types for MINDSPARK:
 *   "story"     -> parallax motion-comic scene (layered landscape + big animated text)
 *   "character" -> "Spark" mascot talking in front of a parallax backdrop (mouth follows narration words)
 * Everything is drawn in code (SVG) from the theme colors, so no images/APIs are needed and
 * every video automatically gets a different look.
 */

type SP = {s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number};

export const STORY_SETTINGS = ["mountains", "city", "forest", "ocean", "night_sky"];
export const MOODS = ["happy", "thinking", "surprised", "determined"];
export const PROPS = ["none", "book", "bulb", "coin"];

const TRANS = 18; // keep in sync with Video.tsx
const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));
const alpha = (hex: string, a: number) => (/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(hex) ? hex + Math.round(a * 255).toString(16).padStart(2, "0") : hex);

const bright = (hex: string) => {
  if (!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(hex)) return "#FFE9A8";
  const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16); const l = (0.299 * (n >> 16) + 0.587 * ((n >> 8) & 255) + 0.114 * (n & 255)) / 255;
  return l > 0.45 ? hex : "#FFE9A8";
};

const hashStr = (str: string) => {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);}
  return h >>> 0;
};
const rng = (seed: number) => () => {
  seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
  let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
  t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
  return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
};

/* ---------- shape generators (viewBox height is always 100, width vw) ---------- */
const smoothRidge = (vw: number, r: () => number, base: number, amp: number, step: number) => {
  const pts: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let x = 0; x <= vw + step; x += step) pts.push([x, base - r() * amp]);
  let d = `M0,100 L0,${pts[0][1].toFixed(1)}`;
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const [px, py] = pts[i - 1]; const [x, y] = pts[i];
    d += ` Q${((px + x) / 2).toFixed(1)},${(Math.min(py, y) - amp * 0.12).toFixed(1)} ${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  return `${d} L${pts[pts.length - 1][0].toFixed(1)},100 Z`;
};
const jaggedRidge = (vw: number, r: () => number, base: number, amp: number, step: number) => {
  let d = `M0,100 L0,${base.toFixed(1)}`;
  for (let x = 0; x <= vw + step; x += step) {
    d += ` L${(x + step * 0.5).toFixed(1)},${(base - amp * (0.45 + 0.55 * r())).toFixed(1)} L${(x + step).toFixed(1)},${(base - amp * 0.12 * r()).toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  return `${d} L${(vw + step * 2).toFixed(1)},100 Z`;
};

type DrawFn = (vw: number, r: () => number, f: number) => React.ReactNode;

const Layer: React.FC<{draw: DrawFn; seed: number; h: number; b: number; sp: number; frames: number; fill: string; op: number; dir?: 1 | -1}> =
  ({draw, seed, h, b, sp, frames, fill, op, dir = 1}) => {
    const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig();
    const u = Math.min(width, height * 0.7) / 100;
    const lh = height * h; const pan = sp * u * frames; const lw = width + pan + 2 * u; const vw = (100 * lw) / lh;
    const off = sp * u * f; const x = dir === 1 ? -off : -(pan - off);
    return (
      <svg width={lw} height={lh} viewBox={`0 0 ${vw.toFixed(1)} 100`} preserveAspectRatio="none"
        style={{position: "absolute", left: 0, bottom: height * b, transform: `translateX(${x.toFixed(1)}px)`, overflow: "visible"}}>
        <g fill={fill} opacity={op}>{draw(vw, rng(seed), f)}</g>
      </svg>
    );
  };

const drawMountains: DrawFn = (vw, r) => <path d={jaggedRidge(vw, r, 80, 70, 14)} />;
const drawHills: DrawFn = (vw, r) => <path d={smoothRidge(vw, r, 70, 40, 18)} />;
const drawFlat: DrawFn = (vw, r) => <path d={smoothRidge(vw, r, 62, 22, 26)} />;

const buildings = (lit: boolean, accent: string): DrawFn => (vw, r, f) => {
  const out: React.ReactNode[] = []; let x = 0; let i = 0;
  while (x < vw + 10) {
    const w = 7 + r() * 8; const hh = 35 + r() * 62; const top = 100 - hh;
    out.push(<rect key={`b${i}`} x={x} y={top} width={w - 0.6} height={hh + 2} />);
    if (lit) {
      for (let wy = top + 4; wy < 94; wy += 6) for (let wx = x + 1.4; wx < x + w - 2.4; wx += 3.2) {
        const on = (Math.floor(wx * 7 + wy * 3 + Math.floor(f / 24)) % 5) < 2;
        if (on) out.push(<rect key={`w${i}-${wx.toFixed(1)}-${wy}`} x={wx} y={wy} width={1.6} height={2.6} fill={accent} opacity={0.9} />);
      }
    }
    x += w; i++;
  }
  return out;
};

const pines = (tall: number): DrawFn => (vw, r) => {
  const out: React.ReactNode[] = []; let x = -4; let i = 0;
  while (x < vw + 8) {
    const w = 7 + r() * 7; const hh = tall * (0.5 + 0.5 * r()); const top = 100 - hh;
    out.push(<path key={i} d={`M${x},100 L${x + w * 0.5},${top} L${x + w},100 Z`} />);
    out.push(<path key={`t${i}`} d={`M${x + w * 0.1},${top + hh * 0.45} L${x + w * 0.5},${top + hh * 0.05} L${x + w * 0.9},${top + hh * 0.45} Z`} />);
    x += w * (0.55 + 0.3 * r()); i++;
  }
  out.push(<rect key="g" x={0} y={92} width={vw + 10} height={10} />);
  return out;
};

const waves = (base: number, amp: number, k: number, speed: number, boat = false): DrawFn => (vw, r, f) => {
  let d = `M0,100 L0,${base}`; const ph = f * speed; const r0 = r() * 6;
  for (let x = 0; x <= vw + 2; x += 2) d += ` L${x},${(base + Math.sin((x + ph + r0) * k) * amp).toFixed(2)}`;
  d += ` L${vw + 2},100 Z`;
  const bx = vw * 0.42; const by = base + Math.sin((bx + ph + r0) * k) * amp;
  return (<>
    <path d={d} />
    {boat && <g transform={`translate(${bx.toFixed(1)} ${(by - 2).toFixed(1)}) rotate(${(Math.sin(f * 0.07) * 4).toFixed(2)})`}>
      <path d="M-7,0 L7,0 L5,4 L-5,4 Z" /><path d="M0,-12 L0,-1 L6,-1 Z" /><path d="M-1,-11 L-1,-1 L-6,-1 Z" opacity={0.7} />
    </g>}
  </>);
};

const clouds: DrawFn = (vw, r) => {
  const out: React.ReactNode[] = []; let x = 0; let i = 0;
  while (x < vw + 20) {
    const cy = 40 + r() * 30; const s = 0.8 + r() * 1.1;
    out.push(<g key={i}><ellipse cx={x} cy={cy} rx={10 * s} ry={5 * s} /><ellipse cx={x + 7 * s} cy={cy - 3 * s} rx={7 * s} ry={5 * s} /><ellipse cx={x - 7 * s} cy={cy - 1.5 * s} rx={6 * s} ry={4 * s} /></g>);
    x += 38 + r() * 40; i++;
  }
  return out;
};

/* ---------- sky, stars, particles ---------- */
const Stars: React.FC<{seed: number; n: number; u: number; top: number}> = ({seed, n, u, top}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig(); const r = rng(seed ^ 0x5bd1e995);
  return (
    <svg width={width} height={height} style={{position: "absolute", inset: 0}}>
      {Array.from({length: n}, (_, i) => {
        const x = r() * width; const y = r() * height * top; const rad = (0.2 + r() * 0.4) * u; const ph = r() * 6;
        return <circle key={i} cx={x} cy={y} r={rad} fill="#fff" opacity={0.25 + 0.7 * Math.abs(Math.sin(f * 0.06 + ph))} />;
      })}
    </svg>
  );
};

const Dust: React.FC<{seed: number; n: number; u: number; color: string; fire?: boolean}> = ({seed, n, u, color, fire}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig(); const r = rng(seed ^ 0x2545f491);
  return (
    <svg width={width} height={height} style={{position: "absolute", inset: 0}}>
      {Array.from({length: n}, (_, i) => {
        const x0 = r() * width; const y0 = r() * height; const sp = 0.15 + r() * 0.4; const ph = r() * 6; const rad = (0.3 + r() * 0.5) * u;
        const y = fire ? height * (0.5 + 0.4 * ((y0 / height + Math.sin(f * 0.02 + ph) * 0.04) % 1)) : ((y0 - f * sp * u * 0.6) % height + height) % height;
        const x = x0 + Math.sin(f * 0.03 + ph) * 3 * u;
        const o = fire ? 0.2 + 0.8 * Math.abs(Math.sin(f * 0.07 + ph)) : 0.35;
        return <circle key={i} cx={x} cy={y} r={rad} fill={color} opacity={o} />;
      })}
    </svg>
  );
};

const Celestial: React.FC<{kind: "sun" | "moon"; x: number; y0: number; y1: number; size: number; frames: number; color: string; u: number}> = ({kind, x, y0, y1, size, frames, color, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig();
  const y = interpolate(f, [0, frames], [y0, y1], {extrapolateRight: "clamp"}) * height; const d = size * u;
  return (
    <div style={{position: "absolute", left: x * width - d / 2, top: y - d / 2, width: d, height: d, borderRadius: "50%", background: color,
      boxShadow: `0 0 ${d * 0.5}px ${d * 0.18}px ${alpha(color, 0.55)}, 0 0 ${d * 1.4}px ${d * 0.4}px ${alpha(color, 0.2)}`}}>
      {kind === "moon" && (<>
        <div style={{position: "absolute", left: "22%", top: "30%", width: "18%", height: "18%", borderRadius: "50%", background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.10)"}} />
        <div style={{position: "absolute", left: "55%", top: "55%", width: "24%", height: "24%", borderRadius: "50%", background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.08)"}} />
      </>)}
    </div>
  );
};

const ShootingStar: React.FC<{start: number; color: string; u: number}> = ({start, color, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig();
  const p = clamp((f - start) / 26); if (p <= 0 || p >= 1) return null;
  const x = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [width * 1.0, width * 0.15]); const y = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [height * 0.08, height * 0.34]);
  return <div style={{position: "absolute", left: x, top: y, width: 22 * u, height: 0.5 * u, transformOrigin: "0 50%", transform: "rotate(-34deg) translateX(0)",
    background: `linear-gradient(90deg, ${color}, transparent)`, opacity: Math.sin(p * Math.PI), boxShadow: `0 0 ${2 * u}px ${color}`}} />;
};

/* ---------- full backdrop ---------- */
const Backdrop: React.FC<{setting: string; seed: number; frames: number; plan: Plan; u: number; dim?: number}> = ({setting, seed, frames, plan, u, dim = 0}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig(); const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const tx = c.tx; const L = (p: {k: string; draw: DrawFn; h: number; b: number; sp: number; op: number; dir?: 1 | -1; fill?: string}) =>
    <Layer key={p.k} draw={p.draw} seed={seed + p.k.length * 977 + Math.round(p.sp * 100)} h={p.h} b={p.b} sp={p.sp} frames={frames} fill={p.fill || tx} op={p.op} dir={p.dir} />;
  const prog = Easing.inOut(Easing.quad)(clamp(f / Math.max(1, frames)));
  let sky: React.ReactNode = null; let layers: React.ReactNode = null; let extra: React.ReactNode = null;

  if (setting === "city") {
    sky = (<><Stars seed={seed} n={36} u={u} top={0.45} /><Celestial kind="moon" x={0.74} y0={0.24} y1={0.2} size={17} frames={frames} color="#FFF6D6" u={u} /></>);
    layers = (<>{L({k: "far", draw: buildings(false, c.ac), h: 0.36, b: 0.24, sp: 0.1, op: 0.16})}{L({k: "mid", draw: buildings(true, c.ac), h: 0.32, b: 0.15, sp: 0.24, op: 0.3, dir: -1})}{L({k: "near", draw: buildings(true, c.ac), h: 0.27, b: 0.04, sp: 0.5, op: 0.55})}</>);
  } else if (setting === "forest") {
    sky = (<><Stars seed={seed} n={22} u={u} top={0.35} /><Celestial kind="moon" x={0.78} y0={0.2} y1={0.17} size={13} frames={frames} color="#FFF0C2" u={u} /></>);
    layers = (<>{L({k: "far", draw: pines(55), h: 0.3, b: 0.27, sp: 0.1, op: 0.16})}{L({k: "mid", draw: pines(70), h: 0.34, b: 0.16, sp: 0.25, op: 0.3, dir: -1})}{L({k: "near", draw: pines(90), h: 0.4, b: 0.0, sp: 0.55, op: 0.6})}</>);
    extra = <Dust seed={seed} n={16} u={u} color={c.ac} fire />;
  } else if (setting === "ocean") {
    sky = <Celestial kind="sun" x={0.5} y0={0.4} y1={0.45} size={26} frames={frames} color={bright(c.ac)} u={u} />;
    layers = (<>{L({k: "w1", draw: waves(55, 3, 0.35, 0.5), h: 0.14, b: 0.34, sp: 0.12, op: 0.16})}{L({k: "w2", draw: waves(50, 4, 0.28, 0.8, true), h: 0.16, b: 0.25, sp: 0.22, op: 0.26, dir: -1})}{L({k: "w3", draw: waves(48, 5, 0.22, 1.1), h: 0.18, b: 0.12, sp: 0.38, op: 0.4})}{L({k: "w4", draw: waves(46, 6, 0.18, 1.5), h: 0.2, b: 0.0, sp: 0.6, op: 0.58, dir: -1})}</>);
  } else if (setting === "night_sky") {
    sky = (<><Stars seed={seed} n={70} u={u} top={0.7} /><Celestial kind="moon" x={0.3} y0={0.3} y1={0.26} size={30} frames={frames} color="#FFF6D6" u={u} /><ShootingStar start={Math.round(frames * 0.35)} color={c.ac} u={u} /></>);
    layers = (<>{L({k: "c1", draw: clouds, h: 0.12, b: 0.52, sp: 0.18, op: 0.1})}{L({k: "c2", draw: clouds, h: 0.14, b: 0.38, sp: 0.32, op: 0.08, dir: -1})}{L({k: "h1", draw: drawHills, h: 0.2, b: 0.08, sp: 0.25, op: 0.3})}{L({k: "h2", draw: drawFlat, h: 0.18, b: 0.0, sp: 0.5, op: 0.55})}</>);
  } else {
    sky = <Celestial kind="sun" x={0.66} y0={0.46} y1={0.36} size={22} frames={frames} color={bright(c.ac)} u={u} />;
    layers = (<>{L({k: "far", draw: drawMountains, h: 0.32, b: 0.27, sp: 0.1, op: 0.15})}{L({k: "mid", draw: drawMountains, h: 0.3, b: 0.18, sp: 0.24, op: 0.28, dir: -1})}{L({k: "hill", draw: drawHills, h: 0.26, b: 0.08, sp: 0.42, op: 0.42})}{L({k: "near", draw: drawFlat, h: 0.22, b: 0.0, sp: 0.62, op: 0.6, dir: -1})}</>);
    extra = <Birds seed={seed} u={u} frames={frames} />;
  }

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{background: `linear-gradient(180deg,${c.bg1},${c.bg2})`}}>
      <AbsoluteFill style={{transform: `scale(${1 + 0.09 * prog}) translateY(${-prog * 1.2 * u}px)`, transformOrigin: "50% 70%"}}>
        {sky}{layers}{extra}
        {setting !== "forest" && <Dust seed={seed + 3} n={14} u={u} color={tx} />}
      </AbsoluteFill>
      {dim > 0 && <AbsoluteFill style={{background: alpha(c.bg1, dim)}} />}
      <AbsoluteFill style={{background: "radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 50%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 55%, rgba(0,0,0,0.32) 100%)"}} />
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

const Birds: React.FC<{seed: number; u: number; frames: number}> = ({seed, u, frames}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {width, height} = useVideoConfig(); const c = "#1B1B3A"; const r = rng(seed ^ 77);
  return (
    <svg width={width} height={height} style={{position: "absolute", inset: 0}}>
      {Array.from({length: 4}, (_, i) => {
        const y0 = (0.14 + r() * 0.16) * height; const delay = i * 14; const p = ((f + delay * 3) / (frames + 60));
        const x = width * 1.1 - p * width * 1.3; const y = y0 + Math.sin(f * 0.08 + i) * 1.5 * u; const flap = Math.sin(f * 0.35 + i * 2) * 1.1 * u; const s = (0.8 + r() * 0.5) * u;
        return <path key={i} d={`M${x - 3 * s},${y - flap} Q${x - 1.5 * s},${y - 1.8 * s} ${x},${y} Q${x + 1.5 * s},${y - 1.8 * s} ${x + 3 * s},${y - flap}`} stroke={c} strokeWidth={0.35 * u} fill="none" opacity={0.55} strokeLinecap="round" />;
      })}
    </svg>
  );
};

/* ---------- "story" scene ---------- */
export const StoryScene: React.FC<SP> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {fps, height} = useVideoConfig(); const c = plan.theme.colors; const d = s.data || {};
  const setting = STORY_SETTINGS.includes(d.setting) ? d.setting : "mountains";
  const text = String(d.text || ""); const words = text.split(" ").filter(Boolean);
  const frames = Math.round(s.durationSec * fps) + TRANS; const seed = hashStr(text + setting);
  const hl = String(d.hl || "").toLowerCase(); let hi = words.findIndex((w) => hl && w.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9%]/g, "") === hl.replace(/[^a-z0-9%]/g, ""));
  if (hi < 0) hi = words.length - 1;
  const left = plan.theme.layout === "left"; const fs = (words.length > 7 ? 8.2 : 9.8) * u;
  const tag = d.tag ? String(d.tag) : "";
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill>
      <Backdrop setting={setting} seed={seed} frames={frames} plan={plan} u={u} />
      <AbsoluteFill style={{background: `linear-gradient(180deg, ${alpha(c.bg1, 0.62)} 0%, ${alpha(c.bg1, 0)} 48%)`}} />
      <div style={{position: "absolute", left: 7 * u, right: 7 * u, top: height * 0.1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: left ? "flex-start" : "center", textAlign: left ? "left" : "center", gap: 3 * u}}>
        {tag && <span style={{fontSize: 3.6 * u, letterSpacing: 0.5 * u, padding: `${0.8 * u}px ${2.4 * u}px`, borderRadius: 99, background: c.ac, color: c.bg1, opacity: clamp(f / 8), transform: `translateY(${(1 - clamp(f / 8)) * -2 * u}px)`}}>{tag}</span>}
        <div style={{fontSize: fs, lineHeight: 1.08, color: c.tx, textShadow: `0 ${0.4 * u}px ${1.4 * u}px rgba(0,0,0,0.28)`}}>
          {words.map((w, i) => {
            const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 6 - i * 4), fps, config: {damping: 11, mass: 0.6}}), 0, 1.15);
            return <span key={i} style={{display: "inline-block", marginRight: 2.2 * u, opacity: clamp(sp * 2), color: i === hi ? c.ac : c.tx, transform: `translateY(${(1 - sp) * 5 * u}px) scale(${0.6 + 0.4 * sp})`}}>{w}</span>;
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

/* ---------- "Spark" mascot ---------- */
const OUT = "#1B1B3A"; const BODY = "#FFD23F"; const SPARK = "#FF7A00";

const Arm: React.FC<{side: "l" | "r"; angle: number}> = ({side, angle}) => {
  const sx = side === "l" ? 34 : 66; const d = side === "l" ? `M${sx},86 Q${sx - 10},96 ${sx - 12},108` : `M${sx},86 Q${sx + 10},96 ${sx + 12},108`;
  const hx = side === "l" ? sx - 12 : sx + 12;
  return (
    <g transform={`rotate(${angle.toFixed(1)} ${sx} 86)`}>
      <path d={d} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={9} strokeLinecap="round" fill="none" />
      <path d={d} stroke={BODY} strokeWidth={5.2} strokeLinecap="round" fill="none" />
      <circle cx={hx} cy={108} r={5.2} fill={BODY} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.8} />
    </g>
  );
};

const Prop: React.FC<{kind: string; f: number}> = ({kind, f}) => {
  const bob = Math.sin(f * 0.1) * 2;
  if (kind === "book") return (
    <g transform={`translate(88 ${50 + bob}) rotate(${Math.sin(f * 0.07) * 6})`}>
      <rect x={-10} y={-8} width={20} height={16} rx={2} fill="#4C6EF5" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.6} />
      <rect x={-8} y={-6} width={14} height={12} rx={1} fill="#fff" />
      <path d="M-6,-2 H4 M-6,1 H4 M-6,4 H1" stroke="#9AA5B1" strokeWidth={1} />
      <rect x={-10} y={-8} width={3.4} height={16} fill="#364FC7" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.2} />
    </g>);
  if (kind === "bulb") return (
    <g transform={`translate(88 ${48 + bob})`}>
      <circle r={15 + Math.sin(f * 0.15) * 2} fill="#FFE066" opacity={0.28} />
      <circle r={8} fill="#FFE066" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.6} />
      <rect x={-4} y={7} width={8} height={5} rx={1.5} fill="#ADB5BD" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.2} />
      <path d="M-2.5,2 Q0,-3 2.5,2" stroke={SPARK} strokeWidth={1.2} fill="none" />
    </g>);
  if (kind === "coin") return (
    <g transform={`translate(88 ${50 + bob})`}>
      <ellipse rx={Math.max(1.5, 9 * Math.abs(Math.cos(f * 0.1)))} ry={9} fill="#F5C518" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.6} />
      <ellipse rx={Math.max(1, 5.5 * Math.abs(Math.cos(f * 0.1)))} ry={5.5} fill="none" stroke="#C99700" strokeWidth={1.2} />
    </g>);
  return null;
};

const Mascot: React.FC<{mood: string; prop: string; f: number; fps: number; words: Scene["words"]; size: number}> = ({mood, prop, f, fps, words, size}) => {
  const t = f / fps; let idx = -1; words.forEach((w, i) => {if (t >= w.s && t <= w.e + 0.04) idx = i;});
  const talk = idx >= 0 ? 0.3 + 0.7 * Math.abs(Math.sin(f * 0.85 + idx * 1.7)) : 0;
  const blinkPhase = (f + 23) % 96; const blink = blinkPhase < 5 ? 1 - Math.sin((blinkPhase / 5) * Math.PI) * 0.92 : 1;
  const bob = Math.sin(f * 0.14) * 1.6; const squash = 1 + Math.sin(f * 0.14 + 1) * 0.012;
  const surprised = mood === "surprised"; const eyeRy = surprised ? 12 : 10; const pr = surprised ? 3.2 : 4.2;
  const look = mood === "thinking" ? [-3.2, -3] : [Math.sin(f * 0.04) * 1.6, Math.sin(f * 0.03) * 0.8];
  let aL = 8; let aR = -8;
  if (mood === "happy") {aL = 112 + Math.sin(f * 0.3) * 20; aR = -(8 + Math.sin(f * 0.12) * 5);}
  else if (mood === "thinking") {aL = 6 + Math.sin(f * 0.1) * 3; aR = -(158 + Math.sin(f * 0.08) * 3);}
  else if (mood === "determined") {aL = 38 + Math.sin(f * 0.2) * 4; aR = -(38 + Math.sin(f * 0.2 + 1) * 4);}
  else if (mood === "surprised") {aL = 128 + Math.sin(f * 0.5) * 7; aR = -(128 + Math.sin(f * 0.5 + 1) * 7);}
  const spark = 1 + Math.sin(f * 0.2) * 0.18;
  const brow = (x: number, side: 1 | -1) => {
    if (mood === "determined") return `M${x - 8 * side},${side === 1 ? 33 : 33} L${x + 8 * side},${40}`;
    if (mood === "surprised") return `M${x - 8},31 Q${x},25 ${x + 8},31`;
    if (mood === "thinking") return side === 1 ? `M${x - 8},35 Q${x},30 ${x + 8},34` : `M${x - 8},38 L${x + 8},37`;
    return `M${x - 8},35 Q${x},31 ${x + 8},35`;
  };
  const mouth = () => {
    if (talk > 0) return (<g><ellipse cx={50} cy={67} rx={6 + talk * 1.5} ry={1.6 + talk * 5.2} fill="#7A1F3D" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.6} />{talk > 0.5 && <ellipse cx={50} cy={69.5} rx={3.4} ry={1.5} fill="#FF8FA3" />}</g>);
    if (surprised) return <ellipse cx={50} cy={68} rx={3.6} ry={4.6} fill="#7A1F3D" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.6} />;
    if (mood === "thinking") return <path d="M44,68 Q50,65 57,68" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />;
    if (mood === "determined") return <path d="M43,67 Q50,71 58,64" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />;
    return <path d="M41,64 Q50,75 59,64" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2.2} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />;
  };
  const Eye: React.FC<{cx: number}> = ({cx}) => (
    <g transform={`translate(${cx} 50) scale(1 ${blink.toFixed(2)}) translate(${-cx} -50)`}>
      <ellipse cx={cx} cy={50} rx={8.6} ry={eyeRy} fill="#fff" stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.8} />
      <circle cx={cx + look[0]} cy={50 + look[1]} r={pr} fill={OUT} />
      <circle cx={cx + look[0] + 1.4} cy={50 + look[1] - 1.5} r={1.3} fill="#fff" />
    </g>
  );
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 130" width={size} height={size * 1.3} style={{overflow: "visible"}}>
      <ellipse cx={50} cy={124} rx={26 - bob} ry={4.5} fill="#000" opacity={0.2} />
      <g transform={`translate(0 ${bob.toFixed(2)}) translate(50 124) scale(1 ${squash.toFixed(3)}) translate(-50 -124)`}>
        <ellipse cx={39} cy={120} rx={8} ry={4} fill={OUT} /><ellipse cx={61} cy={120} rx={8} ry={4} fill={OUT} />
        <rect x={32} y={76} width={36} height={42} rx={17} fill={BODY} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2.2} />
        <path d="M50,86 L53,92 L59,92 L54,96 L56,102 L50,98 L44,102 L46,96 L41,92 L47,92 Z" fill={SPARK} opacity={0.9} />
        <Arm side="l" angle={aL} /><Arm side="r" angle={aR} />
        <line x1={50} y1={22} x2={50} y2={11} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2.4} strokeLinecap="round" />
        <g transform={`translate(50 7) scale(${spark.toFixed(3)})`}>
          <circle r={9} fill={SPARK} opacity={0.25} />
          <path d="M0,-7 L2,-2 L7,0 L2,2 L0,7 L-2,2 L-7,0 L-2,-2 Z" fill={SPARK} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={1.4} strokeLinejoin="round" />
        </g>
        <ellipse cx={50} cy={50} rx={33} ry={30} fill={BODY} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2.2} />
        <ellipse cx={30} cy={61} rx={5} ry={3.2} fill="#FF8FA3" opacity={0.6} /><ellipse cx={70} cy={61} rx={5} ry={3.2} fill="#FF8FA3" opacity={0.6} />
        <Eye cx={37} /><Eye cx={63} />
        <path d={brow(37, 1)} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2.4} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />
        <path d={brow(63, -1)} stroke={OUT} strokeWidth={2.4} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />
        {mouth()}
        <Prop kind={prop} f={f} />
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
};

/* ---------- "character" scene ---------- */
export const CharacterScene: React.FC<SP> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {fps, width, height} = useVideoConfig(); const c = plan.theme.colors; const d = s.data || {};
  const mood = MOODS.includes(d.mood) ? d.mood : "happy"; const prop = PROPS.includes(d.prop) ? d.prop : "none";
  const text = String(d.text || ""); const words = text.split(" ").filter(Boolean);
  const frames = Math.round(s.durationSec * fps) + TRANS; const seed = hashStr(text + mood);
  const setting = STORY_SETTINGS.includes(d.setting) ? d.setting : STORY_SETTINGS[seed % STORY_SETTINGS.length];
  const enter = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 2), fps, config: {damping: 11, mass: 0.8}}), 0, 1.15);
  const pop = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 12), fps, config: {damping: 10, mass: 0.6}}), 0, 1.15);
  const size = Math.min(58 * u, height * 0.3); const left = plan.theme.layout === "left";
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill>
      <Backdrop setting={setting} seed={seed} frames={frames} plan={plan} u={u} dim={0.22} />
      <div style={{position: "absolute", left: 0, right: 0, top: height * 0.1, display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", padding: `0 ${7 * u}px`, boxSizing: "border-box"}}>
        <div style={{position: "relative", maxWidth: width * 0.86, background: c.tx, color: c.bg1, borderRadius: 5 * u, padding: `${3.2 * u}px ${4.4 * u}px`, fontSize: (words.length > 8 ? 6 : 7) * u, lineHeight: 1.14, textAlign: "center",
          boxShadow: `0 ${1 * u}px ${3 * u}px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)`, opacity: clamp(pop * 1.5), transform: `scale(${0.6 + 0.4 * pop})`, transformOrigin: "50% 100%"}}>
          {words.map((w, i) => {const o = clamp((f - 14 - i * 3) / 5); return <span key={i} style={{display: "inline-block", marginRight: 1.6 * u, opacity: o, color: i === words.length - 1 ? c.ac === c.tx ? c.bg1 : c.ac : c.bg1}}>{w}</span>;})}
          <div style={{position: "absolute", left: "50%", bottom: -2.2 * u, width: 4.4 * u, height: 4.4 * u, background: c.tx, transform: "translateX(-50%) rotate(45deg)", borderRadius: 0.6 * u}} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{position: "absolute", left: 0, right: 0, bottom: height * 0.2, display: "flex", justifyContent: left ? "flex-start" : "center", paddingLeft: left ? 12 * u : 0,
        transform: `translateY(${(1 - enter) * 22 * u}px) scale(${0.7 + 0.3 * enter})`, transformOrigin: "50% 100%"}}>
        <Mascot mood={mood} prop={prop} f={f} fps={fps} words={s.words || []} size={size} />
      </div>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

FILE [51/92]: remotion/src/templates/Backdrops.tsx

import React from "react";
import {AbsoluteFill, useCurrentFrame, interpolate} from "remotion";

export const TemplateBackdrop: React.FC<{
  type?: string;
  colors: {bg1: string; bg2: string; ac: string; tx: string; cd: string};
  u: number;
}> = ({type, colors: c, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  if (!type || type === "none") return null;

  if (type === "landscape" || type === "mountains") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <circle cx="540" cy="1150" r="180" fill={c.ac} opacity="0.35" />
          <path d="M-50,1450 L300,1100 L750,1550 L1150,1200 L1150,1920 L-50,1920 Z" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.6" />
          <path d="M-50,1600 L450,1300 L950,1700 L1150,1550 L1150,1920 L-50,1920 Z" fill={c.bg1} opacity="0.8" />
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "city") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <rect x="120" y="1100" width="160" height="820" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.5" />
          <rect x="320" y="980" width="180" height="940" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.7" />
          <polygon points="410,880 370,980 450,980" fill={c.ac} opacity="0.8" />
          <rect x="540" y="1050" width="220" height="870" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.5" />
          <rect x="800" y="1180" width="160" height="740" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.6" />
          {/* Lit windows */}
          {[1120, 1180, 1260, 1340, 1420].map((y, i) => (
            <React.Fragment key={i}>
              <rect x="350" y={y} width="16" height="24" fill={c.ac} opacity={((f + i * 8) % 30 > 10) ? 0.8 : 0.2} />
              <rect x="420" y={y} width="16" height="24" fill={c.ac} opacity={((f + i * 12) % 40 > 15) ? 0.8 : 0.2} />
            </React.Fragment>
          ))}
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "forest") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <path d="M100,1500 L180,1350 L140,1350 L220,1200 L300,1350 L260,1350 L340,1500 Z" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.5" />
          <path d="M700,1450 L780,1300 L740,1300 L820,1150 L900,1300 L860,1300 L940,1450 Z" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.5" />
          {/* Fireflies floating */}
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((idx) => {
            const dy = Math.sin((f + idx * 25) * 0.05) * 40;
            const dx = Math.cos((f + idx * 30) * 0.04) * 30;
            return (
              <circle
                key={idx}
                cx={200 + idx * 140 + dx}
                cy={1000 + idx * 100 + dy}
                r={4 * (0.8 + 0.4 * Math.sin((f + idx * 15) * 0.1))}
                fill={c.ac}
                opacity={0.6 + 0.3 * Math.sin(f * 0.1 + idx)}
              />
            );
          })}
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "ocean") {
    const waveShift = Math.sin(f * 0.05) * 30;
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <path d={`M-50,1350 Q270,${1300 + waveShift} 540,1350 T1130,1350 L1130,1920 L-50,1920 Z`} fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.4" />
          <path d={`M-50,1450 Q270,${1490 - waveShift} 540,1450 T1130,1450 L1130,1920 L-50,1920 Z`} fill={c.ac} opacity="0.2" />
          <path d={`M-50,1550 Q270,${1520 + waveShift} 540,1550 T1130,1550 L1130,1920 L-50,1920 Z`} fill={c.bg1} opacity="0.7" />
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "stars") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <circle cx="850" cy="350" r="100" fill={c.ac} opacity="0.25" />
          <circle cx="820" cy="320" r="80" fill={c.bg1} />
          {/* Constellation stars */}
          {[
            [150, 200], [250, 400], [450, 250], [650, 500], [800, 200],
            [180, 800], [900, 950], [350, 1100], [700, 1300], [200, 1400]
          ].map(([x, y], i) => (
            <circle
              key={i}
              cx={x}
              cy={y}
              r={3.5 + 2 * Math.sin(f * 0.12 + i)}
              fill={c.tx}
              opacity={0.4 + 0.5 * Math.sin(f * 0.1 + i * 2)}
            />
          ))}
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "terminal") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        {/* Subtle scanline CRT effect */}
        <div style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          backgroundImage: "repeating-linear-gradient(0deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.4) 0px, rgba(0,0,0,0.4) 2px, transparent 2px, transparent 4px)",
          opacity: 0.5
        }} />
        {/* Soft radial glow in center */}
        <div style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          background: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 45%, ${c.ac}15 0%, transparent 70%)`
        }} />
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "chalkboard") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <div style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          background: "radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(255,255,255,0.05) 0%, transparent 80%)"
        }} />
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "poster") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <div style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          background: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 40%, ${c.ac}18 0%, transparent 75%)`
        }} />
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "cyber") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="cyberGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor={c.ac} stopOpacity="0" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor={c.ac} stopOpacity="0.4" />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <line x1="0" y1="1200" x2="1080" y2="1200" stroke={c.ac} strokeWidth="2" opacity="0.6" />
          {/* Perspective grid lines */}
          {[-400, -100, 200, 540, 880, 1180, 1480].map((x, i) => (
            <line key={i} x1="540" y1="1200" x2={x} y2="1920" stroke={c.ac} strokeWidth="1.5" opacity="0.3" />
          ))}
          {[1260, 1360, 1500, 1700].map((y, i) => (
            <line key={i} x1="0" y1={y} x2="1080" y2={y} stroke={c.ac} strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.2" />
          ))}
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "gradient_blobs") {
    const shift1 = Math.sin(f * 0.04) * 80;
    const shift2 = Math.cos(f * 0.03) * 60;
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <div style={{
          position: "absolute",
          width: 500,
          height: 500,
          borderRadius: 999,
          background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${c.ac} 0%, transparent 70%)`,
          top: `20%`,
          left: `10%`,
          transform: `translate(${shift1}px, ${shift2}px)`,
          filter: "blur(70px)",
          opacity: 0.35
        }} />
        <div style={{
          position: "absolute",
          width: 600,
          height: 600,
          borderRadius: 999,
          background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${c.bg2} 0%, transparent 70%)`,
          bottom: `20%`,
          right: `5%`,
          transform: `translate(${-shift2}px, ${-shift1}px)`,
          filter: "blur(80px)",
          opacity: 0.45
        }} />
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  if (type === "paper_layers") {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none"}}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 1080 1920" style={{width: "100%", height: "100%", position: "absolute"}}>
          <path d="M-50,300 C300,200 700,450 1150,280 L1150,-50 L-50,-50 Z" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.4" filter="drop-shadow(0 15px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.3))" />
          <path d="M-50,1550 C400,1650 800,1450 1150,1600 L1150,1950 L-50,1950 Z" fill={c.bg2} opacity="0.4" filter="drop-shadow(0 -15px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.3))" />
        </svg>
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  return null;
};

import React from "react";
import {spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";
import {Scene, Plan} from "../Video";

const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));

export const CountdownScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const num = d.rank || d.number || d.index || "01";
  const title = d.title || d.text || "DECISION FATIGUE";
  const sub = d.sub || d.desc || "Eliminate trivial choices before noon";

  const numSp = clamp(spring({frame: f, fps, config: {damping: 8, mass: 0.6}}), 0, 1.2);
  const cardSp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 10), fps, config: {damping: 12}}));

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      alignItems: "center",
      width: "100%",
      maxWidth: 90 * u,
      gap: 2 * u,
      textAlign: "center"
    }}>
      {/* Huge Ranking Number */}
      <div style={{
        fontSize: 22 * u,
        fontWeight: "900",
        lineHeight: 0.9,
        color: c.ac,
        opacity: clamp(numSp),
        transform: `scale(${numSp})`,
        textShadow: `0 0 35px ${c.ac}66`
      }}>
        {num}
      </div>

      {/* Main Content Card */}
      <div style={{
        background: c.cd,
        border: `1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25)`,
        borderRadius: 4 * u,
        padding: 4.5 * u,
        width: "100%",
        opacity: cardSp,

import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame} from "remotion";
import {Scene, Plan} from "../Video";

export const CyberGlitchScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const headline = d.text || d.title || "SYSTEM SHIFT: 10X LEVERAGE";
  const glitchOffset = (f % 15 < 3) ? Math.sin(f) * 6 : 0;
  const scanlineY = (f * 12) % 300;

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "center",
      width: "100%",
      maxWidth: 90 * u,
      textAlign: "center",
      position: "relative"
    }}>
      {/* Glitch HUD header */}
      <div style={{
        fontFamily: "monospace",
        fontSize: 3.4 * u,
        color: c.ac,
        letterSpacing: 3,
        marginBottom: 3 * u,
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 2 * u
      }}>
        <span>[SYS.OVERRIDE]</span>
        <span>•</span>
        <span>NODE://0x7F</span>
      </div>

      {/* Main Glitch Headline */}
      <div style={{position: "relative", display: "inline-block"}}>
        {/* Cyan Shift Layer */}
        {glitchOffset !== 0 && (
          <div style={{
            position: "absolute",
            top: 0,
            left: glitchOffset,
            right: 0,
            fontSize: 8.5 * u,
            fontWeight: "900",
            lineHeight: 1.15,
            color: "#00F0FF",
            opacity: 0.7,
            textTransform: "uppercase"
          }}>
            {headline}
          </div>
        )}
        {/* Red Shift Layer */}
        {glitchOffset !== 0 && (
          <div style={{
            position: "absolute",
            top: 0,
            left: -glitchOffset,
            right: 0,
            fontSize: 8.5 * u,
            fontWeight: "900",
            lineHeight: 1.15,
            color: "#FF2A55",
            opacity: 0.7,
            textTransform: "uppercase"
          }}>
            {headline}
          </div>
        )}
        {/* Main Layer */}
        <div style={{
          fontSize: 8.5 * u,
          fontWeight: "900",
          lineHeight: 1.15,
          color: c.tx,
          textTransform: "uppercase",
          textShadow: `0 0 20px ${c.ac}AA`
        }}>
          {headline}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Cyber Tag */}
      {d.tag && (
        <div style={{
          marginTop: 4 * u,
          padding: `${0.8 * u}px ${2.5 * u}px`,
          border: `1.5px solid ${c.ac}`,
          borderRadius: u,
          fontSize: 3.6 * u,
          fontFamily: "monospace",
          color: c.ac
        }}>
          DATA: {d.tag}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";

const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));

export const HabitTrackerScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const streak = d.streak || "21 DAY STREAK";
  const habits: string[] = d.habits || d.items || [
    "Read 10 Pages Daily",
    "45 Mins Deep Work",
    "Zero Phone First Hour"
  ];

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      gap: 3.5 * u,
      width: "100%",
      maxWidth: 90 * u,
      background: c.cd,
      borderRadius: 4 * u,
      padding: 5 * u,
      border: "1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)",
      boxSizing: "border-box"
    }}>
      {/* Streak Badge */}
      <div style={{
        alignSelf: "center",
        background: "#F59E0B",
        color: "#000",
        padding: `${0.8 * u}px ${3 * u}px`,
        borderRadius: 99,
        fontWeight: "900",
        fontSize: 3.8 * u,
        letterSpacing: 1.5,
        boxShadow: "0 4px 15px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4)"
      }}>
        🔥 {streak}
      </div>

      <div style={{display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2.8 * u, marginTop: u}}>
        {habits.map((h, i) => {
          const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 10 - i * 10), fps, config: {damping: 12}}));
          const checked = f >= 20 + i * 12;
          return (
            <div
              key={i}
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                gap: 3 * u,
                background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",
                padding: `${2 * u}px ${3 * u}px`,
                borderRadius: 2.5 * u,
                opacity: sp,
                transform: `translateX(${(1 - sp) * -8 * u}px)`
              }}
            >
              <div style={{
                width: 4.8 * u,
                height: 4.8 * u,
                borderRadius: 1.2 * u,
                border: `2px solid ${checked ? "#10B981" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.4)"}`,
                background: checked ? "#10B981" : "transparent",
                color: "#FFF",
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                fontWeight: "900",
                fontSize: 3.2 * u,
                transition: "all 0.3s ease"
              }}>
                {checked ? "✓" : ""}
              </div>
              <span style={{fontSize: 4.8 * u, fontWeight: "bold"}}>{h}</span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
