FILE [59/92]: remotion/src/templates/KineticBurstScene.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import {spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";
import {Scene, Plan} from "../Video";

const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));

export const KineticBurstScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const headline = d.text || d.title || "STOP PROCRASTINATING NOW";
  const words = String(headline).split(" ");
  const highlightWord = d.hl || words[Math.floor(words.length / 2)] || "";

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "center",
      width: "100%",
      textAlign: "center",
      gap: 3 * u
    }}>
      {d.tag && (
        <div style={{
          fontSize: 4 * u,
          letterSpacing: 3,
          textTransform: "uppercase",
          padding: `${u}px ${3 * u}px`,
          background: c.ac,
          color: c.bg1,
          borderRadius: 99,
          fontWeight: "900"
        }}>
          {d.tag}
        </div>
      )}

      <div style={{
        display: "flex",
        flexWrap: "wrap",
        justifyContent: "center",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: `${2 * u}px ${3.5 * u}px`,
        maxWidth: 92 * u
      }}>
        {words.map((w, i) => {
          const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - i * 5), fps, config: {damping: 8, mass: 0.5}}), 0, 1.25);
          const isHl = w.toLowerCase() === highlightWord.toLowerCase();
          return (
            <span
              key={i}
              style={{
                fontSize: (isHl ? 12 : 9) * u,
                lineHeight: 1,
                fontWeight: "900",
                display: "inline-block",
                color: isHl ? c.ac : c.tx,
                textTransform: "uppercase",
                opacity: clamp(sp * 2),
                transform: `scale(${0.3 + 0.7 * sp}) translateY(${(1 - sp) * 8 * u}px)`,
                textShadow: isHl ? `0 0 30px ${c.ac}88` : undefined
              }}
            >
              {w}
            </span>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";
import {Scene, Plan} from "../Video";

const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));

export const QuizScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};
  
  const question = d.question || d.text || d.title || "Which habit leads to 37x growth?";
  const options: string[] = d.options || [
    "A) Working 14 hours every single day",
    "B) Getting 1% better every single day",
    "C) Waiting for the perfect inspiration"
  ];
  const correctIdx = typeof d.correct === "number" ? d.correct : 1;
  const revealFrame = 45;
  const isRevealed = f >= revealFrame;

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      gap: 3.5 * u,
      width: "100%",
      maxWidth: 90 * u,
      margin: "0 auto",
      boxSizing: "border-box"
    }}>
      {/* Quiz badge */}
      <div style={{
        alignSelf: "center",
        padding: `${0.8 * u}px ${2.5 * u}px`,
        background: c.ac,
        color: c.bg1,
        borderRadius: 99,
        fontSize: 3.8 * u,
        fontWeight: "bold",
        letterSpacing: 1.5,
        textTransform: "uppercase"
      }}>
        ⚡ POP QUIZ
      </div>

      {/* Question Card */}
      <div style={{
        fontSize: 5.5 * u,
        lineHeight: 1.25,
        fontWeight: "bold",
        padding: 3.5 * u,
        background: c.cd,
        borderRadius: 4 * u,
        border: `1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25)`
      }}>
        {question}
      </div>

      {/* 3 Options */}
      <div style={{display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2.2 * u}}>
        {options.slice(0, 3).map((opt, i) => {
          const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 12 - i * 8), fps, config: {damping: 12}}));
          const isCorrect = i === correctIdx;
          const bgOption = isRevealed && isCorrect ? "rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.35)" : c.cd;
          const borderOption = isRevealed && isCorrect ? "2px solid #2ECC71" : "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15)";
          return (
            <div
              key={i}
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "space-between",
                padding: `${2 * u}px ${3 * u}px`,
                background: bgOption,
                border: borderOption,
                borderRadius: 3 * u,
                fontSize: 4.4 * u,
                opacity: sp,
                transform: `translateX(${(1 - sp) * (i % 2 === 0 ? -12 : 12) * u}px)`
              }}
            >
              <span>{opt}</span>
              {isRevealed && isCorrect && (
                <span style={{
                  background: "#2ECC71",
                  color: "#000",
                  borderRadius: 99,
                  width: 4.5 * u,
                  height: 4.5 * u,
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  fontSize: 3.5 * u,
                  fontWeight: "bold"
                }}>
                  ✓
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {useCurrentFrame} from "remotion";
import {Scene, Plan} from "../Video";

export const TerminalScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const command = d.command || "mindspark --execute protocol";
  const lines: string[] = d.lines || d.items || [
    "STATUS: 1% optimization initiated",
    "ANALYZE: cognitive resistance reduced",
    "RESULT: compound growth 37.78x",
    "VERDICT: execution confirmed [OK]"
  ];

  // Typewriter effect
  const fullText = lines.join("\n");
  const charsToShow = Math.min(fullText.length, Math.floor(f * 2.2));
  const currentOutput = fullText.slice(0, charsToShow);
  const cursorBlink = Math.floor(f / 8) % 2 === 0;

  return (
    <div style={{
      width: "100%",
      maxWidth: 92 * u,
      background: "rgba(10, 14, 22, 0.95)",
      border: "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)",
      borderRadius: 24,
      overflow: "hidden",
      boxShadow: `0 30px 80px rgba(0,0,0,0.85), 0 0 20px ${c.ac}22`,
      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
      color: "#E2E8F0",
      boxSizing: "border-box",
      textAlign: "left"
    }}>
      {/* Terminal Title Bar */}
      <div style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 1.2 * u,
        padding: `${2 * u}px ${3.5 * u}px`,
        background: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)",
        borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)"
      }}>
        <div style={{width: 1.8 * u, height: 1.8 * u, borderRadius: 99, background: "#FF5F56"}} />
        <div style={{width: 1.8 * u, height: 1.8 * u, borderRadius: 99, background: "#FFBD2E"}} />
        <div style={{width: 1.8 * u, height: 1.8 * u, borderRadius: 99, background: "#27C93F"}} />
        <span style={{fontSize: 2.4 * u, marginLeft: 2 * u, color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.45)", fontWeight: 600}}>
          terminal — bash
        </span>
      </div>

      <div style={{padding: `${4 * u}px ${4.5 * u}px`}}>
        {/* Terminal Prompt */}
        <div style={{fontSize: 3.8 * u, color: "#F8FAFC", marginBottom: 3 * u, wordBreak: "break-word"}}>
          <span style={{color: c.ac, fontWeight: 700}}>root@mindspark:~# </span>
          <span style={{color: "#38BDF8"}}>{command}</span>
        </div>

        {/* Output lines */}
        <div style={{
          fontSize: 3.6 * u,
          lineHeight: 1.7,
          whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
          minHeight: 18 * u,
          color: c.ac,
          fontWeight: 600
        }}>
          {currentOutput}
          {cursorBlink && <span style={{background: c.ac, color: "#000", padding: "0 2px"}}> </span>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";
import {Scene, Plan} from "../Video";

const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));

export const TimelineScene: React.FC<{s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const d = s.data || {};

  const milestones: string[] = d.milestones || d.items || [
    "Step 1: Cue Trigger",
    "Step 2: Craving Response",
    "Step 3: Immediate Reward"
  ];

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      gap: 4 * u,
      width: "100%",
      maxWidth: 88 * u,
      position: "relative",
      paddingLeft: 8 * u,
      boxSizing: "border-box"
    }}>
      {/* Vertical Path Line */}
      <div style={{
        position: "absolute",
        left: 2.8 * u,
        top: 2 * u,
        bottom: 2 * u,
        width: 0.8 * u,
        background: `linear-gradient(to bottom, ${c.ac}, ${c.tx})`,
        opacity: 0.5
      }} />

      {milestones.map((ms, i) => {
        const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 8 - i * 12), fps, config: {damping: 12}}));
        return (
          <div
            key={i}
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 3.5 * u,
              opacity: sp,
              transform: `translateX(${(1 - sp) * 10 * u}px)`
            }}
          >
            {/* Marker Node */}
            <div style={{
              width: 5 * u,
              height: 5 * u,
              borderRadius: 99,
              background: c.ac,
              color: c.bg1,
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              justifyContent: "center",
              fontWeight: "900",
              fontSize: 3 * u,
              flexShrink: 0,
              boxShadow: `0 0 15px ${c.ac}`
            }}>
              {i + 1}
            </div>

            {/* Content Card */}
            <div style={{
              background: c.cd,
              padding: `${2 * u}px ${3.5 * u}px`,
              borderRadius: 3 * u,
              fontSize: 4.8 * u,
              fontWeight: "bold",
              border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)",
              width: "100%"
            }}>
              {ms}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE [67/92]: remotion/src/Video.tsx

import React from "react";
import {AbsoluteFill, Easing, Sequence, interpolate, spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from "remotion";
import {loadFont as archivo} from "@remotion/google-fonts/ArchivoBlack";
import {loadFont as space} from "@remotion/google-fonts/SpaceGrotesk";
import {loadFont as anton} from "@remotion/google-fonts/Anton";

const FONT: Record<string, string> = {
  ArchivoBlack: archivo("normal", {weights: ["400"], subsets: ["latin"]}).fontFamily,
  SpaceGrotesk: space("normal", {weights: ["700"], subsets: ["latin"]}).fontFamily,
  Anton: anton("normal", {weights: ["400"], subsets: ["latin"]}).fontFamily,
};

export type Word = {w: string; s: number; e: number};
export type Scene = {type: string; narration: string; durationSec: number; words: Word[]; data: any};
export type TemplateConfig = {
  id: string;
  name?: string;
  backdrop?: string;
  caption_style?: string;
  transition?: string;
  mascot?: boolean;
};
export type Plan = {
  book: {title: string; author: string};
  theme: {
    colors: {bg1: string; bg2: string; ac: string; tx: string; cd: string};
    font: string;
    layout: string;
    pattern: string;
    transition: string;
    chart: string;
    format: {name: string; width: number; height: number};
  };
  template?: TemplateConfig;
  scenes: Scene[];
};
type SP = {s: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number};

const clamp = (v: number, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));
const useU = () => {const {width, height} = useVideoConfig(); return Math.min(width, height * 0.7) / 100;};
const TRANS = 18;

const Pattern: React.FC<{p: string; color: string; u: number}> = ({p, color, u}) => {
  const bg: Record<string, string> = {
    grid: `linear-gradient(${color} 1px,transparent 1px),linear-gradient(90deg,${color} 1px,transparent 1px)`,
    dots: `radial-gradient(${color} ${0.25 * u}px,transparent ${0.35 * u}px)`,
    rays: `repeating-conic-gradient(${color} 0 4deg,transparent 4deg 14deg)`,
    diagonal: `repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,${color} 0 ${0.4 * u}px,transparent ${0.4 * u}px ${4 * u}px)`,
  };
  const size = p === "grid" ? `${9 * u}px ${9 * u}px` : p === "dots" ? `${6 * u}px ${6 * u}px` : undefined;
  return <AbsoluteFill style={{backgroundImage: bg[p] || bg.grid, backgroundSize: size, opacity: 0.12}} />;
};

const Hook: React.FC<SP> = ({s, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const words = String(s.data.title).split(" ");
  return (<>
    <div style={{fontSize: 15 * u, lineHeight: 1}}>
      {words.map((w, i) => {
        const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 8 - i * 4), fps, config: {damping: 9, mass: 0.6}}), 0, 1.2);
        return <span key={i} style={{display: "inline-block", marginRight: 3 * u, opacity: clamp(sp * 2), transform: `translateY(${(1 - sp) * 6 * u}px) scale(${0.4 + 0.6 * sp})`}}>{w}</span>;
      })}
    </div>
    <div style={{fontSize: 4.4 * u, opacity: 0.8}}>{s.data.sub}</div>
  </>);
};

const Curve: React.FC<SP> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors; const d = s.data || {};
  const up = Number(d.up) || 1.01, down = Number(d.down) || 0.99, N = 50;
  const vmax = Math.max(1.5, Math.pow(up, 365));
  const pts = (r: number) => Array.from({length: N + 1}, (_, i) => {const x = i * 2; const v = Math.pow(r, x * 3.65); return [x, 80 - ((v - 1) / (vmax - 1)) * 70] as [number, number];});
  const U = pts(up), D = pts(down);
  const path = (a: [number, number][]) => a.map((p, i) => `${i ? "L" : "M"}${p[0]},${p[1].toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
  const prog = interpolate(f, [10, 75], [0, 1], {extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp", easing: Easing.bezier(0.6, 0, 0.3, 1)});
  const pop = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 78), fps, config: {damping: 8}}), 0, 1.3);
  const st = plan.theme.chart;
  const line: React.CSSProperties = {fill: "none", stroke: c.tx, opacity: 0.55, strokeWidth: 1.8, strokeLinecap: "round", strokeDasharray: 1, strokeDashoffset: 1 - prog};
  const bars = U.filter((_, i) => i % 2 === 0);
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 90" style={{width: 84 * u, overflow: "visible"}}>
      <line x1="0" y1="80" x2="100" y2="80" stroke={c.tx} opacity={0.35} strokeWidth={0.6} />
      <path d={path(D)} pathLength={1} style={line} />
      {st === "bars" ? bars.map((p, i) => {const h = (80 - p[1]) * clamp(prog * bars.length - i); return <rect key={i} x={p[0] - 1.5} y={80 - h} width={3} height={h} fill={c.ac} />;}) : (<>
        {st === "area" && <path d={`${path(U)} L100,80 L0,80 Z`} fill={c.ac} opacity={0.25 * prog} />}
        <path d={path(U)} pathLength={1} style={{...line, stroke: c.ac, opacity: 1}} />
      </>)}
      <text x={2} y={12} fontSize={11 * pop} fill={c.ac} opacity={clamp(pop)}>{d.label}</text>
      {d.labelDown && <text x={98} y={76} fontSize={5 * pop} fill={c.tx} textAnchor="end" opacity={clamp(pop)}>{d.labelDown}</text>}
    </svg>
  );
};

const Dial: React.FC<SP> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const c = plan.theme.colors; const d = s.data || {};
  const items: string[] = (d.items || []).slice(0, 6); const n = Math.max(2, items.length); const seg = 1 / n;
  const center = String(d.center || ""); const fs = 14 * Math.min(1, 9 / Math.max(1, center.length));
  return (<>
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" style={{width: 64 * u}}>
      {Array.from({length: n}, (_, i) => (
        <circle key={i} cx={50} cy={50} r={40} fill="none" stroke={c.ac} strokeWidth={7} pathLength={1}
          strokeDasharray={`${seg - 0.02} ${1 - seg + 0.02}`} transform={`rotate(${(i * 360) / n - 90} 50 50)`} opacity={clamp((f - 8 - i * 10) / 10)} />
      ))}
      <text x={50} y={50 + fs / 3} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={fs} fill={c.tx}>{center}</text>
    </svg>
    <div style={{display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 2 * u, justifyContent: plan.theme.layout === "left" ? "flex-start" : "center"}}>
      {items.map((t, i) => {const o = clamp((f - 14 - i * 10) / 10); return <span key={i} style={{fontSize: 4.6 * u, padding: `${u}px ${2.4 * u}px`, borderRadius: 99, background: c.cd, opacity: o, transform: `translateY(${(1 - o) * 2 * u}px)`}}>{t}</span>;})}
    </div>
  </>);
};

const Card: React.FC<SP> = ({s, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const {fps} = useVideoConfig(); const lines: string[] = ((s.data || {}).lines || []).slice(0, 4);
  return (
    <div style={{background: plan.theme.colors.cd, border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.35)", borderRadius: 6 * u, padding: 5 * u, width: "100%", boxSizing: "border-box", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 * u}}>
      {lines.map((t, i) => {const sp = clamp(spring({frame: Math.max(0, f - 8 - i * 12), fps, config: {damping: 14}})); return <div key={i} style={{fontSize: 6.2 * u, lineHeight: 1.15, opacity: sp, transform: `translateX(${(1 - sp) * -8 * u}px)`}}>{t}</div>;})}
    </div>
  );
};

const Quote: React.FC<SP> = ({s, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame(); const d = s.data || {}; const words = String(d.quote).split(" ");
  return (<>
    <div style={{fontSize: 7.5 * u, lineHeight: 1.12}}>
      {words.map((w, i) => {const o = clamp((f - 6 - i * 4) / 6); return <span key={i} style={{display: "inline-block", marginRight: 2 * u, opacity: o, transform: `translateY(${(1 - o) * 3 * u}px)`}}>{w}</span>;})}
    </div>
    <div style={{fontSize: 4.6 * u, opacity: clamp((f - 6 - words.length * 4) / 10) * 0.8}}>{d.by}</div>
  </>);
};

import {AnimeParallaxScene} from "./components/scenes/AnimeParallaxScene";
import {MotionComicScene} from "./components/scenes/MotionComicScene";
import {StoryScene, CharacterScene} from "./Story";
import {QuizScene} from "./templates/QuizScene";
import {KineticBurstScene} from "./templates/KineticBurstScene";
import {PaperScene} from "./templates/PaperScene";
import {TerminalScene} from "./templates/TerminalScene";
import {StickyNotesScene} from "./templates/StickyNotesScene";
import {ComicPanelsScene} from "./templates/ComicPanelsScene";
import {ChalkboardScene} from "./templates/ChalkboardScene";
import {TimelineScene} from "./templates/TimelineScene";
import {CountdownScene} from "./templates/CountdownScene";
import {BeforeAfterScene} from "./templates/BeforeAfterScene";
import {QuotePosterScene} from "./templates/QuotePosterScene";
import {HabitTrackerScene} from "./templates/HabitTrackerScene";
import {CyberGlitchScene} from "./templates/CyberGlitchScene";
import {PaperCutScene} from "./templates/PaperCutScene";
import {GradientFlowScene} from "./templates/GradientFlowScene";
import {TemplateBackdrop} from "./templates/Backdrops";

const SCENES: Record<string, React.FC<SP>> = {
  hook: Hook,
  curve: Curve,
  dial: Dial,
  card: Card,
  quote: Quote,
  story: StoryScene,
  character: CharacterScene,
  animeParallax: AnimeParallaxScene,
  motionComic: MotionComicScene,
  quiz: QuizScene,
  kineticBurst: KineticBurstScene,
  paper: PaperScene,
  terminal: TerminalScene,
  stickyNotes: StickyNotesScene,
  comicPanels: ComicPanelsScene,
  chalkboard: ChalkboardScene,
  timeline: TimelineScene,
  countdown: CountdownScene,
  beforeAfter: BeforeAfterScene,
  quotePoster: QuotePosterScene,
  habitTracker: HabitTrackerScene,
  cyberGlitch: CyberGlitchScene,
  paperCut: PaperCutScene,
  gradientFlow: GradientFlowScene,
};

const Captions: React.FC<{scene: Scene; plan: Plan; u: number}> = ({scene, plan, u}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps, height} = useVideoConfig();
  const c = plan.theme.colors;
  const t = f / fps;
  let active = -1;
  scene.words.forEach((w, i) => {if (t >= w.s) active = i;});

  // Strict 9:16 safe area: bottom 14%
  const bottom = height * 0.14;
  const capStyle = plan.template?.caption_style || "pill";

  // Modern dynamic 4-5 word sliding window (prevents caption clutter)
  const windowSize = 5;
  const safeActive = active < 0 ? 0 : active;
  const startIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.max(0, scene.words.length - windowSize), safeActive - 2));
  const visibleWords = scene.words.length <= windowSize 
    ? scene.words.map((w, i) => ({...w, origIdx: i}))
    : scene.words.slice(startIdx, startIdx + windowSize).map((w, i) => ({...w, origIdx: startIdx + i}));

  return (
    <div style={{
      position: "absolute",
      left: 8 * u,
      right: 8 * u,
      bottom,
      display: "flex",
      flexWrap: "wrap",
      justifyContent: "center",
      alignItems: "center",
      gap: `${1.2 * u}px ${1.8 * u}px`,
      fontSize: 5.2 * u,
      zIndex: 20
    }}>
      {visibleWords.map((w) => {
        const isActive = w.origIdx === active;
        let badgeStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
          padding: `${0.6 * u}px ${2.2 * u}px`,
          borderRadius: 99,
          opacity: isActive ? 1 : 0.6,
          background: isActive ? c.ac : "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)",
          color: isActive ? c.bg1 : "#F8FAFC",
          transform: isActive ? "scale(1.14)" : "none",
          fontWeight: isActive ? 800 : 600,
          boxShadow: isActive ? `0 4px 20px ${c.ac}44` : "none",
          transition: "all 0.12s ease"
        };

        if (capStyle === "bold") {
          badgeStyle = {
            ...badgeStyle,
            fontWeight: "900",
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            background: isActive ? c.ac : "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)",
            color: isActive ? "#000" : "#FFF"
          };
        } else if (capStyle === "cyber") {
          badgeStyle = {
            ...badgeStyle,
            fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
            borderRadius: 6,
            border: isActive ? `2px solid ${c.ac}` : "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15)",
            color: isActive ? c.ac : "#E2E8F0",
            background: isActive ? "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)"
          };
        } else if (capStyle === "chalk") {
          badgeStyle = {
            ...badgeStyle,
            fontFamily: "monospace",
            border: isActive ? "2px dashed #FDE047" : "none",
            color: isActive ? "#FDE047" : "#FFF"
          };
        } else if (capStyle === "minimal") {
          badgeStyle = {
            ...badgeStyle,
            background: "transparent",
            color: isActive ? c.ac : "#E2E8F0",
            textDecoration: isActive ? "underline" : "none"
          };
        }

        return <span key={w.origIdx} style={badgeStyle}>{w.w}</span>;
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

const SceneWrap: React.FC<{plan: Plan; scene: Scene; index: number}> = ({plan, scene, index}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const u = useU();
  const t = plan.theme;
  const c = t.colors;
  const p = index === 0 ? 1 : interpolate(f, [0, TRANS], [0, 1], {extrapolateRight: "clamp", easing: Easing.bezier(0.7, 0, 0.2, 1)});
  
  let tr: React.CSSProperties;
  const transitionType = plan.template?.transition || t.transition;
  if (transitionType === "wipe") tr = {clipPath: `circle(${p * 150}% at 50% 60%)`};
  else if (transitionType === "slide") tr = {transform: `translateX(${(1 - p) * 100}%)`};
  else if (transitionType === "zoom") tr = {transform: `scale(${1.5 - 0.5 * p})`, opacity: p};
  else tr = {transform: `perspective(${120 * u}px) rotateY(${(1 - p) * -90}deg)`, opacity: p > 0 ? 1 : 0};

  const Comp = SCENES[scene.type] || Hook;
  const left = t.layout === "left";

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{background: `linear-gradient(150deg,${c.bg1},${c.bg2})`, ...tr}}>
      <Pattern p={t.pattern} color={c.tx} u={u} />
      <TemplateBackdrop type={plan.template?.backdrop} colors={c} u={u} />
      <AbsoluteFill style={{
        justifyContent: "center",
        alignItems: left ? "flex-start" : "center",
        textAlign: left ? "left" : "center",
        padding: `8% ${8 * u}px 14% ${8 * u}px`,
        gap: 5 * u,
        color: c.tx,
        fontFamily: FONT[t.font] || FONT.ArchivoBlack,
        boxSizing: "border-box"
      }}>
        <Comp s={scene} plan={plan} u={u} />
      </AbsoluteFill>
      <Captions scene={scene} plan={plan} u={u} />
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

export const Main: React.FC<Plan> = (plan) => {
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  let from = 0;
  const last = plan.scenes.length - 1;
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{background: plan.theme.colors.bg1}}>
      {plan.scenes.map((s, i) => {
        const d = Math.round(s.durationSec * fps);
        const el = <Sequence key={i} from={from} durationInFrames={d + (i < last ? TRANS : 0)}><SceneWrap plan={plan} scene={s} index={i} /></Sequence>;
        from += d;
        return el;
      })}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

FILE [70/92]: scripts/authorize_youtube.py

import os
import sys
import webbrowser
from pathlib import Path

# Enforce UTF-8 for Windows console
if sys.stdout.encoding.lower() != 'utf-8':
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

from config.settings import config
from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import InstalledAppFlow

def main():
    print("==========================================================")
    print("🎬 MINDSPARK YOUTUBE CHANNEL AUTHORIZATION")
    print("==========================================================")

    secrets_file = config.youtube.client_secrets_file
    token_file = config.youtube.token_file

    if not os.path.exists(secrets_file):
        print(f"\n❌ ERROR: {secrets_file} not found!")
        return

    print("Starting local authorization server on port 8080...")
    print("Your browser should open automatically in a few seconds.")
    print("If it does not open, click the URL that will be printed below.\n")

    try:
        flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file(
            secrets_file,
            scopes=config.youtube.scopes
        )
        # Run local server to catch the redirect code
        creds = flow.run_local_server(port=8080, prompt="consent", open_browser=True)

        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(token_file), exist_ok=True)
        with open(token_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(creds.to_json())

        print("\n==========================================================")
        print("✅ SUCCESS! Your YouTube channel is now connected!")
        print(f"Token saved at: {token_file}")
        print("Now running .\\create_next_video.ps1 will upload videos to your channel!")
        print("==========================================================")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n❌ Authorization failed: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import os
import sys
import argparse
import logging
from pathlib import Path

# Enforce UTF-8 for Windows console
if sys.stdout.encoding and sys.stdout.encoding.lower() != 'utf-8':
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
if sys.stderr and sys.stderr.encoding and sys.stderr.encoding.lower() != 'utf-8':
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from src.youtube.youtube_client import youtube_uploader

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s")
logger = logging.getLogger("YouTubeUploadCLI")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Upload any video to YouTube via YouTube Data API v3")
    parser.add_argument("--file", "-f", type=str, help="Path to the video file (.mp4)")
    parser.add_argument("--title", "-t", type=str, default="", help="Title of the video")
    parser.add_argument("--description", "-d", type=str, default="", help="Description of the video")
    parser.add_argument("--tags", type=str, default="", help="Comma-separated tags (e.g. 'Shorts,Motivation,MindSpark')")
    parser.add_argument("--privacy", "-p", type=str, choices=["private", "unlisted", "public"], default="private", help="Privacy status (default: private)")
    parser.add_argument("--thumbnail", type=str, default=None, help="Path to custom thumbnail (.jpg or .png)")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Simulate upload without hitting YouTube API")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    print("=" * 60)
    print("▶️ YOUTUBE VIDEO UPLOADER")
    print("=" * 60)

    # Check OAuth authorization
    if not youtube_uploader.is_configured():
        print("❌ Error: config/client_secrets.json not found.")
        print("Please place your Google Cloud OAuth client_secrets.json in the config/ folder.")
        sys.exit(1)

    if not youtube_uploader.is_authorized():
        print("⚠️ YouTube account is not authorized yet.")
        print("Run: .\\connect_youtube.ps1 (or python scripts/authorize_youtube.py)")
        sys.exit(1)

    # Interactive prompt if video path not passed via arguments
    video_path = args.file
    if not video_path:
        video_path = input("Enter path to video file (.mp4): ").strip().strip('"')

    if not os.path.exists(video_path):
        print(f"❌ Error: Video file not found at: {video_path}")
        sys.exit(1)

    title = args.title.strip()
    if not title:
        default_title = Path(video_path).stem.replace("_", " ").title()
        title_input = input(f"Enter video title [Default: '{default_title}']: ").strip()
        title = title_input if title_input else default_title

    description = args.description.strip()
    if not description:
        description = f"{title}\n\nUploaded via YouTube Automation."

    tags_list = [t.strip() for t in args.tags.split(",") if t.strip()]
    if not tags_list:
        tags_list = ["Shorts", "YouTube", "Video"]

    privacy = args.privacy

    print("\n📋 Upload Details:")
    print(f"   📁 File:        {video_path}")
    print(f"   📝 Title:       {title}")
    print(f"   📄 Description: {description[:60]}...")
    print(f"   🏷️ Tags:        {tags_list}")
    print(f"   🔒 Privacy:     {privacy}")
    print(f"   🖼️ Thumbnail:   {args.thumbnail or 'None'}")
    print(f"   🧪 Dry Run:     {args.dry_run}")
    print("-" * 60)

    print("🚀 Starting upload to YouTube...")
    res = youtube_uploader.upload_video(
        video_path=video_path,
        title=title,
        description=description,
        tags=tags_list,
        privacy_status=privacy,
        thumbnail_path=args.thumbnail,
        dry_run=args.dry_run
    )

    print("=" * 60)
    if res.get("status") in ["success", "dry_run_success"]:
        print("✅ UPLOAD SUCCESSFUL!")
        print(f"   🎥 Video ID:   {res.get('video_id')}")
        print(f"   🔗 Video URL:  {res.get('video_url')}")
        print(f"   🔒 Privacy:    {res.get('privacy')}")
    else:
        print("❌ UPLOAD FAILED!")
        print(f"   Error: {res.get('error')}")
    print("=" * 60)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Original ambient pad ducked at -16 dB under the voice."""
import subprocess
from common import ROOT

def mix(voice, total, out):
    bgm_file = ROOT / "data" / "audio" / "bgm" / "ambient_lofi_pad.mp3"
    if bgm_file.exists():
        fc = (f"[1:a]volume=-16dB,afade=t=in:d=1.5,afade=t=out:st={max(0, total - 2):.2f}:d=2[m];"
              "[0:a][m]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:normalize=0[a]")
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error",
            "-i", str(voice),
            "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", str(bgm_file),
            "-filter_complex", fc,
            "-map", "[a]",
            "-t", str(total),
            "-ar", "44100",
            str(out)
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
    else:
        d = total + 1
        fc = ("[1][2][3]amix=inputs=3:normalize=0,tremolo=f=0.25:d=0.5,volume=-16dB,"
              f"afade=t=in:d=2,afade=t=out:st={max(0, total - 2):.2f}:d=2[m];"
              "[0][m]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:normalize=0[a]")
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", str(voice)]
        for f in (130.81, 164.81, 196.0):
            cmd += ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=f={f}:d={d}"]
        cmd += ["-filter_complex", fc, "-map", "[a]", "-ar", "44100", str(out)]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)

FILE [73/92]: src/common.py

import json, os, pathlib
ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

def load_env():
    p = ROOT / ".env"
    if p.exists():
        for line in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            line = line.strip()
            if line and not line.startswith("#") and "=" in line:
                k, v = line.split("=", 1)
                os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip().strip('"').strip("'"))
    # Fallback mappings for old .env keys
    if "LLM_API_KEY" not in os.environ and "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in os.environ:
        os.environ["LLM_API_KEY"] = os.environ["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"]
    if "LLM_BASE_URL" not in os.environ and "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL" in os.environ:
        os.environ["LLM_BASE_URL"] = os.environ["ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"]
    if "LLM_MODEL" not in os.environ and "ANTHROPIC_MODEL" in os.environ:
        os.environ["LLM_MODEL"] = os.environ["ANTHROPIC_MODEL"]
    if "LLM_API_STYLE" not in os.environ:
        os.environ["LLM_API_STYLE"] = "openai"

def jload(path, default):
    try:
        return json.loads(pathlib.Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return default

def jsave(path, data):
    p = pathlib.Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

================================================================================
FILE [74/92]: src/daily_pipeline.py
================================================================================
"""Daily pipeline: book -> template & theme -> plan (Claude) -> voice+BGM -> Remotion -> final.mp4
Strictly 9:16 vertical 1080x1920 with 22 dynamic visual templates.
"""
import sys, re, random, shutil, argparse, datetime, subprocess, pathlib
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from common import ROOT, load_env, jload, jsave
import theme_engine, template_engine, llm, tts, audio, notify

THEME_HIST = ROOT / "data" / "theme_history.json"
slug = lambda s: re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", s.lower()).strip("-")

def pick_book(history, forced=None):
    books = jload(ROOT / "data" / "books.json", [])
    if forced:
        return next((b for b in books if b["title"].lower() == forced.lower()), {"title": forced, "author": ""})
    cp = ROOT / "completed_books.md"
    done = cp.read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower() if cp.exists() else ""
    used = {h["book"].lower() for h in history if "book" in h}
    left = [b for b in books if b["title"].lower() not in done and b["title"].lower() not in used]
    if not left:
        print("All books from books.json used! Resetting selection pool...")
        left = books
    return random.choice(left)

def verify_resolution(video_path):
    """Verifies that the generated video is strictly 1080x1920 using ffprobe."""
    try:
        cmd = [
            "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0",
            "-show_entries", "stream=width,height",
            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0", str(video_path)
        ]
        out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).decode().strip()
        dims = out.split("x")
        w, h = int(dims[0]), int(dims[1])
        return w, h
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ ffprobe check warning: {e}")
        return 1080, 1920

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--book")
    ap.add_argument("--template")
    ap.add_argument("--headless", action="store_true", help="CI mode (kept for clarity)")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    load_env()
    
    theme_history = jload(THEME_HIST, [])
    template_history = template_engine.load_template_history()
    style_cfg = jload(ROOT / "config" / "style.json", {})
    voice_cfg = jload(ROOT / "config" / "voice_config.json", {})
    
    book = pick_book(theme_history, a.book)
    
    # 1. Pick Template
    if a.template:
        style_cfg["template_mode"] = "fixed"
        style_cfg["fixed_id"] = a.template
        
    template = template_engine.pick_template(history=template_history, config=style_cfg)
    theme = template["theme"]

    # Hard rule: strictly enforce 1080x1920 vertical
    if theme.get("format", {}).get("width") != 1080 or theme.get("format", {}).get("height") != 1920:
        print("⚠️ Non-vertical format requested. Forcing strictly to 1080x1920 vertical.")
        theme["format"] = {"name": "vertical", "width": 1080, "height": 1920}
        theme["formatName"] = "vertical"

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"📖 Book: {book['title']}")
    print(f"🎨 Template: {template['name']} ({template['id']})")
    print(f"✨ Palette: {theme['palette']} | Transition: {theme['transition']} | Layout: {theme['layout']}")
    print("=" * 60)

    # 2. Generate Plan with LLM (with template fallback safety)
    recent_seqs = [h.get("sceneTypes", []) for h in theme_history[-3:] if isinstance(h, dict)]
    try:
        plan = llm.make_plan(book, recent_sequences=recent_seqs, template=template)
    except Exception as plan_err:
        print(f"⚠️ Template plan failed: {plan_err}. Falling back to default classic template.")
        template = template_engine.DEFAULT_TEMPLATE
        theme = template_engine._enrich_template(template, theme_history, random.Random())["theme"]
        plan = llm.make_plan(book, recent_sequences=recent_seqs, template=template)

    unique_ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H%M%S")
    key = f"{unique_ts}_{slug(book['title'])}"
    work, out = ROOT / "data" / "work" / key, ROOT / "data" / "output" / key
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 3. Audio Narration & Mixing
    voice = tts.narrate(plan["scenes"], work, voice_cfg)
    total = sum(s["durationSec"] for s in plan["scenes"])
    audio.mix(voice, total, work / "audio.mp3")

    # 4. Remotion Render
    rp = {
        "book": book,
        "theme": theme,
        "template": {
            "id": template["id"],
            "name": template["name"],
            "backdrop": template.get("backdrop", "none"),
            "caption_style": template.get("caption_style", "pill"),
            "transition": template.get("transition", theme.get("transition", "slide")),
            "mascot": template.get("mascot", False)
        },
        "scenes": plan["scenes"]
    }
    jsave(work / "plan.json", rp)
    silent = work / "silent.mp4"
    npx = shutil.which("npx.cmd") or shutil.which("npx") or "npx.cmd"
    
    subprocess.run([
        npx, "remotion", "render", "src/index.ts", "Main", str(silent),
        f"--props={(work / 'plan.json').resolve().as_posix()}"
    ], cwd=ROOT / "remotion", check=True)

    # 5. Final Video Assembly
    final = out / "final.mp4"
    subprocess.run([
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", str(silent), "-i", str(work / "audio.mp3"),
        "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-shortest", str(final)
    ], check=True)

    # 6. Strict Format Verification with ffprobe
    w, h = verify_resolution(final)
    print(f"📏 Output video dimensions: {w}x{h}")
    if (w, h) != (1080, 1920):
        error_msg = f"❌ Format violation: Video resolution is {w}x{h}, expected 1080x1920!"
        print(error_msg)
        notify.send_msg(error_msg)
        raise RuntimeError(error_msg)

    # 7. Metadata and History Recording
    y = plan["youtube"]
    meta = {
        "book": book["title"],
        "title": y["title"],
        "description": y.get("description", ""),
        "tags": y.get("tags", []),
        "privacy": "private",
        "template_id": template["id"],
        "template_name": template["name"]
    }
    jsave(out / "meta.json", meta)
    jsave(out / "plan.json", rp)

    # Record theme & template history
    theme_history.append({
        "book": book["title"],
        "date": str(datetime.date.today()),
        "theme": theme,
        "template_id": template["id"],
        "sceneTypes": [s["type"] for s in plan["scenes"]]
    })
    jsave(THEME_HIST, theme_history)
    template_engine.record_template_history(template, book["title"])

    shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)

    # 8. Send to Telegram for 1-Click Mobile Review
    caption = (
        f"{y['title']}\n\n"
        f"🎨 Template: *{template['name']}* (`{template['id']}`)\n"
        f"✨ Palette: {theme['palette']} / {theme['transition']} / 9:16 Vertical"
    )
    notify.send(final, caption, video_dir=out)
    print("DONE:", final)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE [75/92]: src/export_project_to_text.py
================================================================================
"""Exports the entire MindSpark project codebase and configuration into a single text file.
Strictly excludes sensitive credentials (.env, tokens, secrets) and binary media assets.
"""
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".git", "node_modules", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache",
    ".idea", ".vscode", "venv", ".venv", "build", "dist"
}

EXCLUDE_FILES = {
    ".env", "client_secrets.json", "youtube_token.json",
    "package-lock.json"
}

ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py", ".ts", ".tsx", ".json", ".yml", ".yaml",
    ".md", ".txt", ".ini", ".toml", ".html", ".css", ".js"
}

EXCLUDE_PATH_SUBSTRINGS = [
    os.path.join("data", "work"),
    os.path.join("data", "output"),
    os.path.join("remotion", "node_modules"),
]

def should_include(rel_path: Path) -> bool:
    rel_str = str(rel_path)
    # Check directory exclusion
    for part in rel_path.parts[:-1]:
        if part in EXCLUDE_DIRS:
            return False
    # Check path substrings
    for sub in EXCLUDE_PATH_SUBSTRINGS:
        if sub in rel_str:
            return False
    # Check file exclusion
    if rel_path.name in EXCLUDE_FILES:
        return False
    # Check extension
    if rel_path.suffix.lower() not in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS:
        return False
    # Don't include the output text file itself
    if rel_path.name in ("mindspark_project.txt", "project_codebase.txt"):
        return False
    return True

def generate_export():
    out_file = ROOT / "mindspark_project.txt"
    files_to_export = []

    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(ROOT):
        # Filter directories in-place
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in EXCLUDE_DIRS and not any(sub in os.path.join(root, d) for sub in EXCLUDE_PATH_SUBSTRINGS)]
        for f in files:
            full_path = Path(root) / f
            rel_path = full_path.relative_to(ROOT)
            if should_include(rel_path):
                files_to_export.append(rel_path)

    files_to_export.sort()

    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write("MINDSPARK STUDIO - COMPLETE CODEBASE ARCHIVE\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write(f"Total Files Included: {len(files_to_export)}\n")
        out.write("Generated for: MindSpark automated Hinglish YouTube Shorts Studio\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        out.write("TABLE OF CONTENTS:\n")
        for i, fp in enumerate(files_to_export, 1):
            out.write(f"{i:3d}. {fp.as_posix()}\n")
        out.write("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        for i, fp in enumerate(files_to_export, 1):
            full_path = ROOT / fp
            out.write("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")
            out.write(f"FILE [{i}/{len(files_to_export)}]: {fp.as_posix()}\n")
            out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
            try:
                content = full_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                out.write(content)
                if not content.endswith("\n"):
                    out.write("\n")
            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"[ERROR READING FILE: {e}]\n")

    print(f"Successfully generated archive with {len(files_to_export)} files at:")
    print(out_file)
    print(f"File size: {out_file.stat().st_size / 1024:.1f} KB")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_export()

"""Generates demo plans for each of the 22 templates in data/demo_plan_<template_id>.json."""

}

def generate_demo_plans():
    templates = template_engine.load_templates()
    print(f"Generating demo plans for {len(templates)} templates...")
    
    out_dir = ROOT / "data"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    generated = []

    for t in templates:
        tid = t["id"]
        enriched = template_engine._enrich_template(t, [], None)
        recipe = t.get("scene_recipe", ["hook", "card", "quote"])
        
        scenes = []
        for s_type in recipe:
            words = [{"w": w, "s": i * 0.4, "e": (i + 1) * 0.4} for i, w in enumerate("This is a demo caption for testing".split())]
            data = SCENE_MOCK_DATA.get(s_type, {"text": "Default Insight"})
            scenes.append({
                "type": s_type,
                "narration": "Ye ek atomic habits ka powerful lesson hai jo aapki zindagi badal dega.",
                "durationSec": 2.5,
                "words": words,
                "data": data
            })

        plan = {
            "book": SAMPLE_BOOK,
            "theme": enriched["theme"],
            "template": {
                "id": t["id"],
                "name": t["name"],
                "backdrop": t.get("backdrop", "none"),
                "caption_style": t.get("caption_style", "pill"),
                "transition": t.get("transition", "slide"),
                "mascot": t.get("mascot", False)
            },
            "scenes": scenes,
            "youtube": {
                "title": f"{SAMPLE_BOOK['title']} | {t['name']} Shorts",
                "description": f"Master lessons from {SAMPLE_BOOK['title']} with {t['name']}.",
                "tags": ["shorts", "mindspark", t["id"]]
            }
        }

        file_path = out_dir / f"demo_plan_{tid}.json"
        jsave(file_path, plan)
        generated.append(file_path)

    print(f"✅ Generated {len(generated)} demo plans in {out_dir}/")
    return generated

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_demo_plans()

FILE [77/92]: src/llm.py
================================================================================
"""Asks Claude (claude-sonnet-5 via your relay) for a VIDEO PLAN as JSON.
Supports 22 templates with recipe matching, deep normalization, and 9:16 vertical enforcement.
"""
import os, sys, json, requests
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from common import ROOT, jload

SYSTEM = "You are the script writer of a Hinglish book-lessons YouTube Shorts studio. Reply with ONE JSON object only. No markdown, no explanation."

TYPES = {
    "hook", "curve", "dial", "card", "quote", "story", "character", "animeParallax", "motionComic",
    "quiz", "kineticBurst", "paper", "terminal", "stickyNotes", "comicPanels", "chalkboard",
    "timeline", "countdown", "beforeAfter", "quotePoster", "habitTracker", "cyberGlitch",
    "paperCut", "gradientFlow"
}

SETTINGS = ["mountains", "city", "forest", "ocean", "night_sky"]
MOODS = ["happy", "thinking", "surprised", "determined"]
PROPS = ["none", "book", "bulb", "coin"]
STYLES = ("mix", "story", "character", "classic", "anime")

NEED = {
    "hook": ["title"],
    "curve": ["up", "down", "label"],
    "dial": ["center", "items"],
    "card": ["lines"],
    "quote": ["quote"],
    "story": ["setting", "text"],
    "character": ["mood", "text"],
    "animeParallax": ["title", "emotion"],
    "motionComic": ["panel1Title", "panel2Title"],
    "quiz": ["question", "options"],
    "kineticBurst": ["text"],
    "paper": ["title", "points"],
    "terminal": ["command", "lines"],
    "stickyNotes": ["notes"],
    "comicPanels": ["panel1Title", "panel2Title"],
    "chalkboard": ["title", "items"],
    "timeline": ["milestones"],
    "countdown": ["rank", "title"],
    "beforeAfter": ["before", "after"],
    "quotePoster": ["quote"],
    "habitTracker": ["streak", "habits"],
    "cyberGlitch": ["text"],
    "paperCut": ["title", "desc"],
    "gradientFlow": ["text", "sub"],
}

def _call(prompt):
    base = os.environ.get("LLM_BASE_URL", "https://api.llmsrelay.com").rstrip("/")
    if base.endswith("/v1"):
        base = base[:-3]
    key, model = os.environ.get("LLM_API_KEY", ""), os.environ.get("LLM_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-5")
    if not key:
        raise RuntimeError("LLM_API_KEY is not set.")
        
    if os.environ.get("LLM_API_STYLE", "openai") == "anthropic":
        r = requests.post(base + "/v1/messages", timeout=180,
            headers={"x-api-key": key, "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"},
            json={"model": model, "max_tokens": 3000, "system": SYSTEM, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]})
        r.raise_for_status()
        return r.json()["content"][0]["text"]
    r = requests.post(base + "/v1/chat/completions", timeout=180, headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + key},
        json={"model": model, "max_tokens": 3000, "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM}, {"role": "user", "content": prompt}]})
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

def get_style():
    """config/style.json -> legacy style or template_mode"""
    cfg = jload(ROOT / "config" / "style.json", {})
    st = str(cfg.get("animation_style", "story")).lower()
    return st if st in STYLES else "story"

def normalize(p):
    """Fix small LLM slips so daily runs never fail on missing fields."""
    if not isinstance(p, dict):
        return p
    scenes = p.get("scenes", [])
    for idx, s in enumerate(scenes):
        if not isinstance(s, dict):
            continue
        d = s.get("data")
        if not isinstance(d, dict):
            d = {}
            s["data"] = d
            
        stype = s.get("type")
        if stype == "story":
            if d.get("setting") not in SETTINGS:
                d["setting"] = SETTINGS[len(str(d.get("text", ""))) % len(SETTINGS)]
            if not d.get("text"):
                d["text"] = "Power of intentional focus"
        elif stype == "character":
            if d.get("mood") not in MOODS: d["mood"] = "happy"
            if d.get("prop") not in PROPS: d["prop"] = "none"
            if not d.get("text"): d["text"] = "Consistency beats intensity!"
        elif stype == "quiz":
            if not d.get("question"): d["question"] = "Which habit leads to massive results?"
            if not d.get("options") or not isinstance(d.get("options"), list):
                d["options"] = ["A) Waiting for luck", "B) 1% daily improvement", "C) Overthinking"]
            if "correct" not in d: d["correct"] = 1
        elif stype == "kineticBurst":
            if not d.get("text"): d["text"] = "MASSIVE SHIFT HAPPENS DAILY"
        elif stype == "paper":
            if not d.get("title"): d["title"] = "Core Principle"
            if not d.get("points") or not isinstance(d.get("points"), list):
                d["points"] = ["Focus deeply", "Remove distractions", "Measure execution"]
        elif stype == "terminal":
            if not d.get("command"): d["command"] = "system.optimize()"
            if not d.get("lines") or not isinstance(d.get("lines"), list):
                d["lines"] = ["TASK: Compound Protocol", "STATUS: Executing", "RESULT: 37x Compounding"]
        elif stype == "stickyNotes":
            if not d.get("notes") or not isinstance(d.get("notes"), list):
                d["notes"] = [
                    {"text": "Small daily wins", "color": "#FEF08A", "rot": -2},
                    {"text": "Protect your energy", "color": "#FBCFE8", "rot": 2},
                    {"text": "Standardize before optimizing", "color": "#BAE6FD", "rot": -1}
                ]
        elif stype in ("comicPanels", "motionComic"):
            if not d.get("panel1Title"): d["panel1Title"] = "OLD HABIT"
            if not d.get("panel1Subtitle"): d["panel1Subtitle"] = "Endless distractions"
            if not d.get("panel2Title"): d["panel2Title"] = "NEW LEVERAGE"
            if not d.get("panel2Subtitle"): d["panel2Subtitle"] = "Uninterrupted focus blocks"
        elif stype == "chalkboard":
            if not d.get("title"): d["title"] = "CLASSROOM PROTOCOL"
            if not d.get("items") or not isinstance(d.get("items"), list):
                d["items"] = ["Master the fundamentals", "Eliminate friction", "Track your streak"]
        elif stype == "timeline":
            if not d.get("milestones") or not isinstance(d.get("milestones"), list):
                d["milestones"] = ["Phase 1: Identify Trigger", "Phase 2: Routine Action", "Phase 3: Immediate Reward"]
        elif stype == "countdown":
            if not d.get("rank"): d["rank"] = f"0{idx}"
            if not d.get("title"): d["title"] = "DECISION MASTERY"
        elif stype == "beforeAfter":
            if not d.get("before"): d["before"] = "Multitasking chaos"
            if not d.get("after"): d["after"] = "Single-task focus blocks"
        elif stype in ("quote", "quotePoster"):
            if not d.get("quote"): d["quote"] = "Small disciplines repeated with consistency lead to great achievements."
        elif stype == "habitTracker":
            if not d.get("streak"): d["streak"] = "30 DAY PROTOCOL"
            if not d.get("habits") or not isinstance(d.get("habits"), list):
                d["habits"] = ["Morning Routine", "45-min Deep Focus", "Daily Review"]
        elif stype == "cyberGlitch":
            if not d.get("text"): d["text"] = "UPGRADE OPERATING SYSTEM"
        elif stype == "paperCut":
            if not d.get("title"): d["title"] = "Depth Over Noise"
            if not d.get("desc"): d["desc"] = "Build systems that outlast daily motivation."
        elif stype == "gradientFlow":
            if not d.get("text"): d["text"] = "The Power of Momentum"
            if not d.get("sub"): d["sub"] = "Friction decreases as velocity increases."
            
    # Guarantee strict 9:16 vertical 1080x1920 format
    if "theme" in p and isinstance(p["theme"], dict):
        p["theme"]["format"] = {"name": "vertical", "width": 1080, "height": 1920}
        p["theme"]["formatName"] = "vertical"

    return p

def validate(p, template=None, style=None):
    sc = p.get("scenes")
    if not isinstance(sc, list) or not 4 <= len(sc) <= 7:
        return "need 4 to 7 scenes"
    if sc[0].get("type") != "hook":
        return "first scene must be hook"
    if sc[-1].get("type") not in ("card", "quote", "quotePoster"):
        return "last scene must be card, quote, or quotePoster"
    if len({s.get("type") for s in sc}) < 3:
        return "need at least 3 different scene types"
    
    for s in sc:
        t = s.get("type")
        if t not in TYPES:
            return f"unknown type {t}"
        if not str(s.get("narration", "")).strip():
            return "empty narration"
        for k in NEED.get(t, []):
            if k not in s.get("data", {}):
                return f"{t} scene missing data.{k}"
                
    # Validate template recipe adherence if template provided
    if template and isinstance(template, dict):
        recipe = template.get("scene_recipe", [])
        recipe_types = set(recipe)
        present_types = set(s.get("type") for s in sc)
        # Ensure at least the core specialized scene types from the recipe are present
        specialized = recipe_types - {"hook", "card", "quote"}
        if specialized and not (present_types & specialized):
            return f"template {template.get('id')} requires at least one of {specialized}"

    if not p.get("youtube", {}).get("title"):
        return "missing youtube.title"
        
    return None

def make_plan(book, recent_sequences=None, template=None):
    style = get_style()
    template_info = ""
    if template and isinstance(template, dict):
        t_id = template.get("id")
        t_name = template.get("name")
        t_desc = template.get("description")
        t_recipe = template.get("scene_recipe", [])
        template_info = f"""\nACTIVE TEMPLATE: {t_name} ({t_id})
Template Look & Vibe: {t_desc}
TARGET SCENE RECIPE: {json.dumps(t_recipe)}
Ensure your chosen scenes closely match this recipe while covering the book's core insights."""
    else:
        template_info = f"\nSTYLE RULE: {STYLE_RULE.get(style, 'Use rich varied scenes')}"

    prompt = f"""Book: {book['title']} by {book.get('author', 'Author')}.
Make a 25-35 second vertical 9:16 video plan about its 3-4 most powerful lessons.
Return JSON: {{"scenes":[{{"type":..., "narration":..., "data":{{...}}}}], "youtube":{{"title":..., "description":..., "tags":[...]}}}}
{template_info}

Available Scene types and their data:
- hook: {{"title":"max 4 words CAPS", "sub":"{book['title']}, {book.get('author', 'Author')}"}}
- story: animated parallax landscape. {{"setting":"mountains"|"city"|"forest"|"ocean"|"night_sky", "text":"punchy Hinglish line", "hl":"word to highlight", "tag":"e.g. LESSON 1"}}
- character: Spark mascot speech bubble. {{"mood":"happy"|"thinking"|"surprised"|"determined", "prop":"none"|"book"|"bulb"|"coin", "text":"Hinglish speech bubble"}}
- quiz: interactive quiz. {{"question":"quiz question", "options":["A) ...", "B) ...", "C) ..."], "correct":1}}
- kineticBurst: bold typography explosion. {{"text":"PUNCHY HIGH ENERGY LINE", "hl":"WORD", "tag":"INSIGHT"}}
- paper: clean minimalist note. {{"title":"Core Rule", "points":["point 1", "point 2", "point 3"]}}
- terminal: retro hacker console. {{"command":"sys.run()", "lines":["line 1", "line 2", "line 3"]}}
- stickyNotes: pinned notes board. {{"notes":[{{"text":"Note 1", "color":"#FEF08A", "rot":-2}}, {{"text":"Note 2", "color":"#FBCFE8", "rot":2}}]}}
- comicPanels: split comic strip. {{"panel1Title":"OLD WAY", "panel1Subtitle":"bad habit", "panel2Title":"NEW PROTOCOL", "panel2Subtitle":"exponential result"}}
- chalkboard: classroom chalk board. {{"title":"RULE", "items":["point 1", "point 2"]}}
- timeline: roadmap journey. {{"milestones":["Step 1", "Step 2", "Step 3"]}}
- countdown: numbered ranking. {{"rank":"01", "title":"LESSON", "sub":"explanation"}}
- beforeAfter: split comparison. {{"before":"old trap", "after":"breakthrough shift"}}
- quotePoster: editorial quote poster. {{"quote":"powerful insight quote", "by":"{book.get('author', 'Author')}"}}
- habitTracker: daily habit streak. {{"streak":"30 DAY PROTOCOL", "habits":["habit 1", "habit 2", "habit 3"]}}
- cyberGlitch: glitch cyber digital HUD. {{"text":"SYSTEM SHIFT", "tag":"PROTOCOL"}}
- paperCut: organic layered paper craft. {{"title":"Depth Over Noise", "desc":"meaningful insight"}}
- gradientFlow: modern kinetic gradient flow. {{"text":"MOMENTUM", "sub":"formula for action"}}
- curve: growth compounding chart. {{"up":1.01, "down":0.99, "label":"37x", "labelDown":"0.03x"}}
- dial: circular pillar breakdown. {{"center":"PILLARS", "items":["item 1", "item 2", "item 3"]}}
- card: takeaway cards. {{"lines":["takeaway 1", "takeaway 2", "takeaway 3"]}}
- quote: wisdom quote. {{"quote":"quote text", "by":"{book.get('author', 'Author')}"}}

Rules: 5 or 6 scenes. First scene = hook. Last scene = card, quote, or quotePoster. At least 3 different scene types.
Narration: natural Hinglish (Roman Hindi + English), 12-22 words per scene, high attention hook, no emojis.
Avoid these recent scene sequences: {json.dumps(recent_sequences or [])}.
youtube.title under 70 chars, description 2 short lines + hashtags."""

    err = None
    for _ in range(3):
        txt = _call(prompt + (f"\nYour previous answer was invalid: {err}. Fix it." if err else ""))
        try:
            plan = json.loads(txt[txt.index("{"): txt.rindex("}") + 1])
            plan = normalize(plan)
            err = validate(plan, template=template, style=style)
            if not err:
                return plan
        except Exception as e:
            err = f"not valid JSON ({e})"
    raise RuntimeError("LLM plan failed: " + str(err))

STYLE_RULE = {
    "mix": "include at least 1 story scene AND at least 1 character scene.",
    "story": "include at least 2 story scenes and NO character scene.",
    "character": "include at least 2 character scenes and NO story scene.",
    "classic": "do NOT use story or character scenes.",
    "anime": "include at least 1 animeParallax or motionComic scene.",
}

FILE [78/92]: src/notify.py

"""Sends video directly to Telegram as streamable MP4 with 1-click PASS / REJECT buttons."""
import os, time, json, requests
from pathlib import Path

def send(video, caption, video_dir=None):
    t, c = os.environ.get("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN"), os.environ.get("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID")
    if not (t and c):
        return
    video_path = Path(video)
    if not video_path.exists():
        return

    # Inline keyboard buttons for 1-tap mobile review
    dir_name = Path(video_dir).name if video_dir else video_path.parent.name
    reply_markup = {
        "inline_keyboard": [
            [
                {"text": "🚀 PASS ", "callback_data": f"pass:{dir_name}"},
                {"text": "❌ REJECT", "callback_data": f"reject:{dir_name}"}
            ]
        ]
    }

    full_caption = (
        f"🎬 *New Reel Ready for Review!*\n\n"
        f"{caption}\n\n"
        f"👉 Tap *PASS* button  to upload immediately to YouTube!"
    )[:1000]

    with open(video_path, "rb") as f:
        try:
            r = requests.post(
                f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{t}/sendVideo",
                timeout=300,
                data={
                    "chat_id": c,
                    "caption": full_caption,
                    "parse_mode": "Markdown",
                    "supports_streaming": "true",
                    "reply_markup": json.dumps(reply_markup)
                },
                files={"video": f}
            )
            if not r.ok:
                # If markdown fails due to special chars, send as plain text
                f.seek(0)
                r = requests.post(
                    f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{t}/sendVideo",
                    timeout=300,
                    data={
                        "chat_id": c,
                        "caption": caption[:900],
                        "supports_streaming": "true",
                        "reply_markup": json.dumps(reply_markup)
                    },
                    files={"video": f}
                )

            # Record pending video info so telegram_bot can pre-load or instant PASS
            if r.ok:
                resp_data = r.json()
                res = resp_data.get("result", {})
                file_id = res.get("video", {}).get("file_id")
                file_unique_id = res.get("video", {}).get("file_unique_id")
                msg_id = res.get("message_id")
                
                # Check for meta.json
                target_video_dir = Path(video_dir) if video_dir else video_path.parent
                meta_path = target_video_dir / "meta.json"
                meta_data = {}
                if meta_path.exists():
                    try:
                        with open(meta_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as mf:
                            meta_data = json.load(mf)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                
                pending_info = {
                    "dir_name": dir_name,
                    "file_id": file_id,
                    "file_unique_id": file_unique_id,
                    "message_id": msg_id,
                    "caption": full_caption,
                    "meta": meta_data,
                    "timestamp": int(time.time()),
                    "status": "pending_review"
                }
                
                root_data = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data"
                root_data.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
                with open(root_data / "pending_video.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as pf:
                    json.dump(pending_info, pf, indent=2)
                print(f"[Telegram Notify] Video sent. Saved data/pending_video.json for dir '{dir_name}'")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Telegram Notify] Error sending video: {e}")

FILE [79/92]: src/render_template_demos.py

"""Renders demo video for each of the 22 templates with Remotion.
Verifies ffprobe dimensions (must be 1080x1920), records render time, and prints Markdown table.
"""
import os, sys, time, json, shutil, subprocess
from pathlib import Path

if sys.stdout and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
        sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from common import ROOT, jload
import template_engine

def get_video_dimensions(video_path):
    try:
        cmd = [
            "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0",
            "-show_entries", "stream=width,height",
            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0", str(video_path)
        ]
        out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).decode().strip()
        dims = out.split("x")
        return f"{dims[0]}x{dims[1]}"
    except Exception as e:
        return "Unknown"

def run_all_renders():
    templates = template_engine.load_templates()
    print(f"=== Starting Remotion Renders for {len(templates)} Templates ===\n")
    
    npx = shutil.which("npx.cmd") or shutil.which("npx") or "npx.cmd"
    test_out_dir = ROOT / "data" / "test_renders"
    test_out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    results = []

    for idx, t in enumerate(templates, 1):
        tid = t["id"]
        tname = t["name"]
        plan_file = ROOT / "data" / f"demo_plan_{tid}.json"
        out_video = test_out_dir / f"demo_{tid}.mp4"
        
        if not plan_file.exists():
            results.append((tid, tname, "FAIL", "0.0s", "Missing plan file"))
            continue

        print(f"[{idx:02d}/{len(templates):02d}] Rendering template: {tid} ({tname})...")
        t0 = time.time()
        
        # Render first 60 frames (2 seconds) to thoroughly test React component mounts, transitions & backdrops
        cmd = [
            npx, "remotion", "render", "src/index.ts", "Main",
            str(out_video),
            f"--props={plan_file.resolve().as_posix()}",
            "--frames=0-60",
            "--log=error"
        ]
        
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT / "remotion", capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
            elapsed = time.time() - t0
            dims = get_video_dimensions(out_video)
            status = "OK" if dims == "1080x1920" else "FAIL (Dimensions)"
            results.append((tid, tname, status, f"{elapsed:.1f}s", dims))
            print(f"       -> Status: {status} | Time: {elapsed:.1f}s | Size: {dims}")
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as err:
            elapsed = time.time() - t0
            err_msg = (err.stderr or err.stdout or "Render error")[:120].strip()
            print(f"       -> FAIL: {err_msg}")
            results.append((tid, tname, "FAIL", f"{elapsed:.1f}s", "Render Failed"))

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("### TEMPLATE RENDER VERIFICATION TABLE")
    print("=" * 80)
    header = f"| # | Template ID | Template Name | Render Status | Render Time | Output Resolution |"
    sep = f"|---|-------------|---------------|---------------|-------------|-------------------|"
    print(header)
    print(sep)
    for i, r in enumerate(results, 1):
        print(f"| {i:02d} | `{r[0]}` | {r[1]} | **{r[2]}** | {r[3]} | `{r[4]}` |")
    print("=" * 80)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all_renders()

"""Builds a standalone mobile review website for GitHub Pages or static hosting."""

from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
OUT = ROOT / "data" / "output"
PUBLIC = ROOT / "public"

HTML_TEMPLATE = """<!DOCTYPE html>

      <span>⚡ MindSpark Studio</span>
    </div>
    <span class="badge">Live Review</span>
  </div>

  <div class="container">

        Your browser does not support video playback.

    <div class="card">

      <div class="meta-desc">__DESC__</div>

        __TAGS__
      </div>
    </div>

    <div id="statusBox" class="status-msg"></div>

      <a href="video.mp4" download="mindspark_daily.mp4" class="btn btn-download">
        ⬇️ Save Video
      </a>
      <button class="btn btn-fail" onclick="rejectVideo()">
        ✕ Discard
      </button>
      <button class="btn btn-pass" onclick="approveAndUpload()">
        🚀 PASS & UPLOAD TO YOUTUBE
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    const runId = "__RUN_ID__";
    const repo = "ITvALA/youtube_autometion";

    function showStatus(msg, isSuccess) {
      const box = document.getElementById("statusBox");
      box.style.display = "block";
      box.style.background = isSuccess ? "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)" : "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)";
      box.style.color = isSuccess ? "#34D399" : "#F87171";
      box.style.border = isSuccess ? "1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3)" : "1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)";
      box.innerHTML = msg;
    }

    async function approveAndUpload() {
      let token = localStorage.getItem("gh_token");
      if (!token) {
        token = prompt("Enter your GitHub Personal Access Token (with workflow permission) to trigger upload:");
        if (token) localStorage.setItem("gh_token", token);
        else return;
      }
      showStatus("⏳ Triggering YouTube upload workflow in GitHub Actions...", true);
      try {
        const resp = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/repos/${repo}/actions/workflows/approve.yml/dispatches`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + token,
            "Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json",
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({ ref: "main", inputs: { run_id: runId || "latest" } })
        });
        if (resp.ok) {
          showStatus("✅ SUCCESS! Upload triggered! Video will appear on YouTube channel in ~60 seconds.", true);
        } else {
          showStatus("⚠️ GitHub API error: " + resp.status + ". Token may lack workflow permissions.", false);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showStatus("⚠️ Request error: " + err.message, false);
      }
    }

    function rejectVideo() {
      if (confirm("Reject this video?")) {
        showStatus("❌ Video rejected. It will not be published.", false);
      }
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""

def build_site(run_id=""):
    PUBLIC.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dirs = sorted(OUT.glob("*/meta.json"))
    if not dirs:
        print("No videos found in data/output/")
        return
    latest = dirs[-1].parent
    meta = json.loads((latest / "meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if (latest / "meta.json").exists() else {}
    video_path = latest / "final.mp4"
    if video_path.exists():
        shutil.copy2(video_path, PUBLIC / "video.mp4")
        print(f"Copied {video_path} to {PUBLIC / 'video.mp4'} ({video_path.stat().st_size} bytes)")

    title = meta.get("title", "Daily MindSpark Shorts")
    desc = meta.get("description", "")
    tags_html = "".join(f'<span class="tag">#{t}</span>' for t in meta.get("tags", []))

    html = HTML_TEMPLATE.replace("__TITLE__", title)
    html = html.replace("__DESC__", desc)
    html = html.replace("__TAGS__", tags_html)
    html = html.replace("__RUN_ID__", str(run_id))

    (PUBLIC / "index.html").write_text(html, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Generated standalone mobile review site at {PUBLIC / 'index.html'}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_id = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ""
    build_site(run_id)
