import os
import sys
import json
import shutil
import socket
from http.server import HTTPServer, SimpleHTTPRequestHandler
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse
from pathlib import Path

# Ensure UTF-8 output on Windows consoles
if sys.stdout.encoding and sys.stdout.encoding.lower() != 'utf-8':
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
if sys.stderr and sys.stderr.encoding and sys.stderr.encoding.lower() != 'utf-8':
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
SRC_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "src"
if str(SRC_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC_DIR))

from common import ROOT, load_env, jload, jsave
import youtube_upload

HOST = "0.0.0.0"
PORT = 8080
OUT = ROOT / "data" / "output"

def get_local_ip():
    """Finds local network IP so the user can open it on their phone."""
    try:
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        s.connect(("8.8.8.8", 80))
        ip = s.getsockname()[0]
        s.close()
        return ip
    except Exception:
        return "127.0.0.1"

class MobileReviewHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    def do_HEAD(self):
        self.do_GET()

    def do_GET(self):
        parsed = urlparse(self.path)
        path = parsed.path

        # 1. API: Get Current Video Review State
        if path == "/api/state":
            dirs = sorted(OUT.glob("*/meta.json"))
            if dirs:
                latest = dirs[-1].parent
                meta = jload(latest / "meta.json", {})
                plan = jload(latest / "plan.json", {})
                scenes = plan.get("scenes", [])
                sections = []
                for idx, s in enumerate(scenes):
                    sections.append({
                        "chapter_title": f"Scene {idx+1} ({s.get('type', '')})",
                        "hindi_chapter_title": s.get("type", "").upper(),
                        "headline": s.get("narration", "")[:80] + ("..." if len(s.get("narration", "")) > 80 else "")
                    })
                total_duration = round(sum(s.get("durationSec", 6.0) for s in scenes) / 60.0, 1)
                state = {
                    "status": "ready_for_review",
                    "ready_time": meta.get("created_at", "Today"),
                    "book_title": meta.get("title", meta.get("book", latest.name)),
                    "english_title": meta.get("book", latest.name),
                    "author": meta.get("author", plan.get("book", {}).get("author", "MindSpark")),
                    "category": plan.get("theme", {}).get("formatName", "Vertical Video (9:16)"),
                    "core_concept": f"Palette: {plan.get('theme', {}).get('palette', '')} | Trans: {plan.get('theme', {}).get('transition', '')}",
                    "duration_minutes": total_duration or 0.6,
                    "video_url": f"/video/{latest.name}/final.mp4",
                    "folder": latest.name,
                    "script_data": {"sections": sections}
                }
            else:
                state = {
                    "status": "waiting_generation",
                    "message": "No pending videos found in data/output. Run python src/daily_pipeline.py"
                }

            cfg_path = ROOT / "config" / "voice_config.json"
            if cfg_path.exists():
                try:
                    state["voice_config"] = jload(cfg_path, {})
                except Exception:
                    pass

            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(json.dumps(state, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"))
            return

        # 2. Serve Audio Files
        if path.startswith("/audio/voice/"):
            voice_file = path.replace("/audio/voice/", "")
            v_path = OUT / "voices" / voice_file
            if v_path.exists():
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "audio/mpeg")
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(v_path.stat().st_size))
                self.end_headers()
                with open(v_path, "rb") as f:
                    self.wfile.write(f.read())
            else:
                self.send_error(404, "Voice Audio Not Found")
            return

        if path == "/audio/bgm":
            bgm_path = ROOT / "data" / "audio" / "bgm" / "ambient_lofi_pad.mp3"
            if bgm_path.exists():
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "audio/mpeg")
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(bgm_path.stat().st_size))
                self.end_headers()
                with open(bgm_path, "rb") as f:
                    self.wfile.write(f.read())
            else:
                self.send_error(404, "BGM Not Found")
            return

        # 3. Serve Dynamic & Sample Videos
        if path.startswith("/video/") and path.endswith("/final.mp4"):
            parts = path.strip("/").split("/")
            if len(parts) == 3:
                folder_name = parts[1]
                v_path = OUT / folder_name / "final.mp4"
                if v_path.exists():
                    self.send_response(200)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", "video/mp4")
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(v_path.stat().st_size))
                    self.end_headers()
                    if self.command != "HEAD":
                        with open(v_path, "rb") as f:
                            shutil.copyfileobj(f, self.wfile)
                    return
            self.send_error(404, "Rendered Video Not Found")
            return

        if path.startswith("/video/sample_"):
            clean_name = path.replace("/video/", "") + ".mp4"
            sample_file = OUT / "samples" / clean_name
            if sample_file.exists():
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "video/mp4")
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(sample_file.stat().st_size))
                self.end_headers()
                if self.command != "HEAD":
                    with open(sample_file, "rb") as f:
                        shutil.copyfileobj(f, self.wfile)
            else:
                self.send_error(404, f"Sample Video '{clean_name}' Not Found")
            return

        # 4. Main HTML UI
        if path in ["/", "/index.html"]:
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(self._render_mobile_ui().encode("utf-8"))
            return

        super().do_GET()

    def do_POST(self):
        parsed = urlparse(self.path)
        path = parsed.path
        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        body = self.rfile.read(length).decode("utf-8") if length > 0 else ""
        data = {}
        if body:
            try:
                data = json.loads(body)
            except Exception:
                data = {k: v[0] for k, v in parse_qs(body).items()}

        folder_name = data.get("folder")
        if not folder_name:
            dirs = sorted(OUT.glob("*/meta.json"))
            if dirs:
                folder_name = dirs[-1].parent.name

        target_dir = OUT / folder_name if folder_name else None

        # API: PASS Action
        if path in ["/api/pass", "/pass"]:
            if not target_dir or not target_dir.exists():
                self._send_json({"error": "No pending video found to pass."}, 400)
                return
            try:
                if data.get("title") or data.get("description") or data.get("privacy"):
                    meta = jload(target_dir / "meta.json", {})
                    meta.update({
                        "title": data.get("title", meta.get("title", "")),
                        "description": data.get("description", meta.get("description", "")),
                        "privacy": data.get("privacy", meta.get("privacy", "private"))
                    })
                    jsave(target_dir / "meta.json", meta)

                dry = data.get("dry_run", False) or os.environ.get("DRY_RUN", "").lower() in ("1", "true")
                vid = youtube_upload.approve(target_dir, dry_run=dry)
                self._send_json({
                    "status": "passed_and_uploaded",
                    "youtube_url": f"https://youtu.be/{vid}" if vid else "Archived",
                    "video_id": vid
                })
            except Exception as e:
                self._send_json({"error": str(e)}, 500)
            return

        # API: FAIL Action
        if path in ["/api/fail", "/fail"]:
            if target_dir and target_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(target_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            self._send_json({"status": "failed_and_discarded"})
            return

        # API: Select Voice
        if path == "/api/select_voice":
            voice_id = data.get("voice_id", "")
            voice_name = data.get("voice_name", "")
            cfg_path = ROOT / "config" / "voice_config.json"
            cfg = jload(cfg_path, {})
            cfg["selected_voice_id"] = voice_id
            cfg["selected_voice_name"] = voice_name
            jsave(cfg_path, cfg)
            self._send_json({"status": "ok", "saved": cfg})
            return

        self.send_error(404, "Endpoint Not Found")

    def _send_json(self, payload: dict, status=200):
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"))

    def _render_mobile_ui(self) -> str:
        local_ip = get_local_ip()
        return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="hi">

  <div class="actions-grid">
    <button class="btn btn-pass" onclick="handlePass()">
      <span>✅ PASS</span>

  <!-- SCENE BREAKDOWN -->

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- EDIT MODAL -->
  <div id="editModal">
    <div class="modal-sheet">
      <div class="modal-title">✏️ EDIT METADATA BEFORE UPLOAD</div>

        <input type="text" id="editTitle" value="">

      </div>
      <div class="field">

          <option value="unlisted">Unlisted (सिर्फ लिंक वाले)</option>

        </select>

      <div class="modal-btns">
        <button class="btn btn-pass" style="flex: 2;" onclick="saveAndPass()">🚀 Upload to YouTube</button>
        <button class="btn btn-fail" style="flex: 1;" onclick="closeEditModal()">Cancel</button>
      </div>

  <div id="toast"></div>

  <script>
    let currentState = {{}};

    async function loadState() {{
      try {{
        const res = await fetch('/api/state');
        const data = await res.json();
        currentState = data;
        if (data.status === 'ready_for_review') {{
          document.getElementById('bookTitle').textContent = data.book_title;
          document.getElementById('bookAuthor').textContent = `✍️ ${{data.author}} • ⏱️ ${{data.duration_minutes}} mins`;
          document.getElementById('bookConcept').textContent = `💡 ${{data.core_concept || 'Daily Master Breakdown'}}`;
          document.getElementById('editTitle').value = `${{data.book_title}} - Summary (${{data.author}}) #MindSpark`;
          document.getElementById('editDesc').value = `📚 ${{data.book_title}} by ${{data.author}}.\\n\\n${{data.core_concept}}\\n\\nSubscribe for daily book videos!\\n#Books #MindSpark`;

          const p = document.getElementById('player');
          p.src = data.video_url;
          p.load();

          const list = document.getElementById('chaptersList');
          list.innerHTML = '';
          const sections = data.script_data?.sections || [];
          sections.forEach((s, idx) => {{
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'chapter-card';
            div.innerHTML = `
              <div class="ch-num">${{idx + 1}}</div>
              <div>
                <strong>${{s.hindi_chapter_title || s.chapter_title}}</strong>
                <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #A8A29E; margin-top: 2px;">${{s.headline}}</div>
              </div>
            `;
            list.appendChild(div);
          }});
          document.querySelectorAll('.sample-tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
          document.getElementById('tab_daily').classList.add('active');
        }} else {{
          document.getElementById('bookTitle').textContent = 'No Pending Video';
          document.getElementById('bookConcept').textContent = data.message || 'Run pipeline to generate a video.';
        }}
      }} catch (e) {{
        console.error(e);
      }}
    }}

    function showToast(msg, bg = '#10B981') {{
      const t = document.getElementById('toast');
      t.textContent = msg;
      t.style.background = bg;
      t.style.display = 'block';
      setTimeout(() => {{ t.style.display = 'none'; }}, 3500);
    }}

    async function handlePass() {{
      showToast('🚀 Uploading to YouTube & archiving book...', '#3B82F6');
      try {{
        const res = await fetch('/api/pass', {{
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
          body: JSON.stringify({{ folder: currentState.folder }})
        }});
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === 'passed_and_uploaded') {{
          showToast('✅ Uploaded to YouTube! Book archived.', '#10B981');
          setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 2500);
        }} else {{
          showToast('❌ Upload failed: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'), '#EF4444');
        }}
      }} catch (e) {{
        showToast('❌ Network error', '#EF4444');
      }}
    }}

    async function handleFail() {{
      if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to discard this video?')) return;
      try {{
        await fetch('/api/fail', {{
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
          body: JSON.stringify({{ folder: currentState.folder }})
        }});
        showToast('🗑️ Video discarded.', '#EF4444');
        setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 1500);
      }} catch (e) {{}}
    }}

    function openEditModal() {{
      document.getElementById('editModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }}

    function closeEditModal() {{
      document.getElementById('editModal').style.display = 'none';
    }}

    async function saveAndPass() {{
      const title = document.getElementById('editTitle').value;
      const desc = document.getElementById('editDesc').value;
      const privacy = document.getElementById('editPrivacy').value;
      closeEditModal();
      showToast('🚀 Uploading edited video to YouTube...', '#3B82F6');
      try {{
        const res = await fetch('/api/pass', {{
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
          body: JSON.stringify({{ folder: currentState.folder, title, description: desc, privacy }})
        }});
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === 'passed_and_uploaded') {{
          showToast('✅ Uploaded! Saved to completed_books.md', '#10B981');
          setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 2500);
        }} else {{
          showToast('❌ Error: ' + (data.error || 'Failed'), '#EF4444');
        }}
      }} catch (e) {{
        showToast('❌ Upload error', '#EF4444');
      }}
    }}

    function switchSampleVideo(videoSrc, title, concept, tabId) {{
      const p = document.getElementById('player');
      p.src = videoSrc;
      p.load();
      p.play().catch(e => {{}});
      document.getElementById('bookTitle').textContent = title;
      document.getElementById('bookAuthor').textContent = '🎙️ Voice 5 (Prabhat) • 🎵 Ambient Lo-Fi BGM Mixed In';
      document.getElementById('bookConcept').textContent = '✨ ' + concept;
      document.querySelectorAll('.sample-tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
      const activeBtn = document.getElementById(tabId);
      if (activeBtn) activeBtn.classList.add('active');
      showToast('🎬 ' + title, '#10B981');
    }}

    async function selectVoice(voiceId, voiceName) {{
      showToast('🎙️ Setting ' + voiceName + '...', '#3B82F6');
      try {{
        const res = await fetch('/api/select_voice', {{
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
          body: JSON.stringify({{ voice_id: voiceId, voice_name: voiceName }})
        }});
        const d = await res.json();
        if (d.status === 'ok') {{
          showToast('✅ Selected: ' + voiceName, '#10B981');
          location.reload();
        }}
      }} catch (e) {{
        showToast('❌ Error setting voice', '#EF4444');
      }}
    }}

    loadState();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""

def run_mobile_server(port: int = PORT):
    load_env()
    server = HTTPServer((HOST, port), MobileReviewHandler)
    local_ip = get_local_ip()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("📱 MINDSPARK MOBILE REVIEW APP READY!")
    print(f"👉 Local Access:   http://localhost:{port}")
    print(f"📲 Phone Access:   http://{local_ip}:{port}")
    print("=" * 60)
    server.serve_forever()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_mobile_server()

================================================================================
FILE [82/92]: src/telegram_bot.py
================================================================================
"""Interactive Telegram Bot for MindSpark Studio:
- Instant feedback on PASS button click with live progress bar animation
- Live status updates during YouTube upload
- Auto-preloads pending videos in background into data/output/
- Zero-delay, multithreaded operation
"""
import os, sys, time, json, pathlib, requests, threading, subprocess
from pathlib import Path

if sys.stdout and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
        sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from common import ROOT, load_env, jload, jsave
import youtube_upload

load_env()
TOKEN = os.environ.get("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
ALLOWED_CHAT = str(os.environ.get("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", ""))
BASE_URL = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TOKEN}"
OUT_DIR = ROOT / "data" / "output"
PENDING_FILE = ROOT / "data" / "pending_video.json"

def send_msg(chat_id, text, reply_markup=None):
    data = {"chat_id": chat_id, "text": text, "parse_mode": "Markdown"}
    if reply_markup:
        data["reply_markup"] = json.dumps(reply_markup)
    try:
        r = requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/sendMessage", data=data, timeout=20)
        return r.json().get("result", {})
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error sending msg: {e}")
        return {}

def edit_msg(chat_id, message_id, text, reply_markup=None):
    data = {"chat_id": chat_id, "message_id": message_id, "text": text, "parse_mode": "Markdown"}
    if reply_markup:
        data["reply_markup"] = json.dumps(reply_markup)
    try:
        requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/editMessageText", data=data, timeout=15)
    except Exception:
        pass

def edit_reply_markup(chat_id, message_id, reply_markup):
    try:
        requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/editMessageReplyMarkup", json={
            "chat_id": chat_id,
            "message_id": message_id,
            "reply_markup": reply_markup
        }, timeout=15)
    except Exception:
        pass

def get_progress_bar(percent):
    clamped = max(0, min(100, int(percent)))
    filled = clamped // 10
    empty = 10 - filled
    return "▓" * filled + "░" * empty

def get_latest_output_dir():
    dirs = sorted([d for d in OUT_DIR.glob("*") if d.is_dir() and (d / "final.mp4").exists()], key=os.path.getmtime)
    return dirs[-1] if dirs else None

def download_video_from_telegram(file_id, target_dir):
    """Downloads video directly from Telegram servers into local target_dir."""
    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_file = target_dir / "final.mp4"
    if out_file.exists() and out_file.stat().st_size > 500000:
        return True
    
    file_info = requests.get(f"{BASE_URL}/getFile?file_id={file_id}", timeout=30).json()
    if not file_info.get("ok"):
        raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to get file path from Telegram: {file_info.get('description')}")
    
    file_path = file_info["result"]["file_path"]
    download_url = f"https://api.telegram.org/file/bot{TOKEN}/{file_path}"
    
    with requests.get(download_url, stream=True, timeout=120) as r:
        r.raise_for_status()
        with open(out_file, "wb") as f:
            for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=262144):
                if chunk:
                    f.write(chunk)
    return True

def background_video_sync():
    """Background worker that continuously pulls git updates and pre-downloads pending videos into data/output/."""
    last_git_pull = 0
    while True:
        try:
            now = time.time()
            if now - last_git_pull > 45:
                last_git_pull = now
                try:
                    subprocess.run(
                        ["git", "pull", "--rebase", "-X", "ours", "origin", "main"],
                        cwd=ROOT,
                        stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                        stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                        timeout=30
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if PENDING_FILE.exists():
                pending = jload(PENDING_FILE, {})
                dir_name = pending.get("dir_name")
                file_id = pending.get("file_id")
                meta = pending.get("meta", {})

                if dir_name and file_id:
                    target_dir = OUT_DIR / dir_name
                    target_file = target_dir / "final.mp4"
                    if not target_file.exists() or target_file.stat().st_size < 500000:
                        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                        download_video_from_telegram(file_id, target_dir)
                        if meta and not (target_dir / "meta.json").exists():
                            jsave(target_dir / "meta.json", meta)
        except Exception:
            pass
        time.sleep(10)

def handle_pass_async(chat_id, target_dir_name=None, message_obj=None):
    """Handles upload in background thread with live animated progress bar."""
    if target_dir_name:
        target_dir = OUT_DIR / target_dir_name
    else:
        target_dir = get_latest_output_dir()
    
    # 1. Immediate visual feedback: Update original video button
    orig_msg_id = message_obj.get("message_id") if message_obj else None
    if orig_msg_id:
        edit_reply_markup(chat_id, orig_msg_id, {
            "inline_keyboard": [[{"text": "⏳ Work in progress... ⚙️", "callback_data": "noop"}]]
        })

    # If not found locally on disk, download directly from Telegram message or pending_video.json
    if not target_dir or not (target_dir / "final.mp4").exists():
        video_obj = None
        caption_text = ""
        if message_obj:
            video_obj = message_obj.get("video")
            caption_text = message_obj.get("caption", "")
            if not video_obj and "reply_to_message" in message_obj:
                video_obj = message_obj["reply_to_message"].get("video")
                caption_text = message_obj["reply_to_message"].get("caption", "")

        file_id_to_use = video_obj.get("file_id") if video_obj else None
        
        if not file_id_to_use and PENDING_FILE.exists():
            pending = jload(PENDING_FILE, {})
            if not target_dir_name or pending.get("dir_name") == target_dir_name:
                file_id_to_use = pending.get("file_id")
                target_dir_name = target_dir_name or pending.get("dir_name")

        if file_id_to_use:
            dir_name = target_dir_name or f"reel_{int(time.time())}"
            target_dir = OUT_DIR / dir_name
            try:
                download_video_from_telegram(file_id_to_use, target_dir)
                if not (target_dir / "meta.json").exists():
                    lines = [l.strip() for l in caption_text.split("\n") if l.strip() and not l.startswith("🎬") and not l.startswith("👉")]
                    raw_title = lines[0] if lines else dir_name.replace("-", " ").title()
                    clean_title = raw_title.replace("*", "").strip()[:90]
                    meta = {
                        "book": clean_title,
                        "title": clean_title,
                        "description": caption_text or clean_title,
                        "tags": ["shorts", "reels", "motivation", "books", "mindspark"],
                        "privacy": "public"
                    }
                    jsave(target_dir / "meta.json", meta)
            except Exception as dl_err:
                send_msg(chat_id, f"❌ *Download Failed:*\n`{str(dl_err)}`")
                return
        else:
            send_msg(chat_id, "⚠️ No pending video found. Please tap the *PASS* button directly on the video message.")
            return

    meta = jload(target_dir / "meta.json", {})
    title = meta.get("title", target_dir.name)

    # 2. Send Live Animated Progress Status Message
    status_text = (
        f"⚙️ *WORK IN PROGRESS: Uploading to YouTube*\n\n"
        f"📌 *Video:* `{target_dir.name}`\n"
        f"🎬 *Title:* {title}\n\n"
        f"📊 *Progress:* `[{get_progress_bar(10)}] 10%`\n"
        f"💬 *Status:* Connecting to YouTube Studio API..."
    )
    status_msg = send_msg(chat_id, status_text)
    status_msg_id = status_msg.get("message_id")

    # 3. Live Progress Callback (throttled to 1.5s to respect Telegram limits)
    last_update = [time.time()]
    def on_progress(pct):
        now = time.time()
        if (now - last_update[0] < 1.5) and pct < 100:
            return
        last_update[0] = now
        stage = "Uploading video chunks..." if pct < 85 else "Finalizing metadata & processing..."
        bar = get_progress_bar(pct)
        updated_text = (
            f"⚙️ *WORK IN PROGRESS: Uploading to YouTube*\n\n"
            f"📌 *Video:* `{target_dir.name}`\n"
            f"🎬 *Title:* {title}\n\n"
            f"📊 *Progress:* `[{bar}] {pct}%`\n"
            f"💬 *Status:* {stage}"
        )
        if status_msg_id:
            edit_msg(chat_id, status_msg_id, updated_text)

    # 4. Execute YouTube Upload
    try:
        on_progress(25)
        vid_id = youtube_upload.upload(target_dir, progress_callback=on_progress)
        on_progress(100)
        
        yt_url = f"https://youtu.be/{vid_id}" if vid_id and vid_id != "dry_run_video_id" else "Uploaded (Draft)"
        youtube_upload.append_completed_book(meta.get("book", target_dir.name), "", "Self Improvement", yt_url)
        
        final_success_text = (
            f"🎉 *SUCCESS! Video is LIVE on YouTube!*\n\n"
            f"🎬 *Watch:* {yt_url}\n"
            f"📌 *Title:* {title}\n"
            f"📊 *Progress:* `[{get_progress_bar(100)}] 100% Complete` ✅"
        )
        if status_msg_id:
            edit_msg(chat_id, status_msg_id, final_success_text)
        else:
            send_msg(chat_id, final_success_text)

        # Update original video button with direct YouTube link
        if orig_msg_id and yt_url.startswith("http"):
            edit_reply_markup(chat_id, orig_msg_id, {
                "inline_keyboard": [[{"text": "🎬 Watch on YouTube ↗", "url": yt_url}]]
            })

    except Exception as e:
        err_text = f"❌ *Upload Failed:*\n`{str(e)}`"
        if status_msg_id:
            edit_msg(chat_id, status_msg_id, err_text)
        else:
            send_msg(chat_id, err_text)

def handle_reject(chat_id, target_dir_name=None, message_obj=None):
    if message_obj and message_obj.get("message_id"):
        edit_reply_markup(chat_id, message_obj["message_id"], {
            "inline_keyboard": [[{"text": "❌ REJECTED", "callback_data": "noop"}]]
        })
    send_msg(chat_id, "❌ *Video marked as rejected.* It will not be uploaded to YouTube.")

def start_bot():
    if not TOKEN:
        print("Error: TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN not found in environment or .env file.")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    print("🚀 MindSpark Telegram Bot is online and listening!")
    
    # Start background pre-loader thread
    sync_thread = threading.Thread(target=background_video_sync, daemon=True)
    sync_thread.start()
    print("⚡ Auto-preload background sync active: videos save to output automatically on arrival.")

    offset = 0
    while True:
        try:
            r = requests.get(f"{BASE_URL}/getUpdates", params={"offset": offset, "timeout": 30}, timeout=45)
            if not r.ok:
                time.sleep(3)
                continue
            data = r.json()
            for update in data.get("result", []):
                offset = update["update_id"] + 1
                
                # 1. Handle Inline Button Clicks
                if "callback_query" in update:
                    cb = update["callback_query"]
                    cb_data = cb.get("data", "")
                    sender_chat = str(cb["message"]["chat"]["id"])
                    
                    if ALLOWED_CHAT and sender_chat != ALLOWED_CHAT:
                        continue
                        
                    # Immediate instant feedback via toast notification
                    if cb_data.startswith("pass"):
                        requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/answerCallbackQuery", json={
                            "callback_query_id": cb["id"],
                            "text": "🚀 Upload shuru ho gaya! Work in progress..."
                        })
                        dir_name = cb_data.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in cb_data else None
                        threading.Thread(
                            target=handle_pass_async,
                            args=(sender_chat, dir_name, cb.get("message")),
                            daemon=True
                        ).start()
                    elif cb_data.startswith("reject"):
                        requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/answerCallbackQuery", json={
                            "callback_query_id": cb["id"],
                            "text": "❌ Video rejected."
                        })
                        dir_name = cb_data.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in cb_data else None
                        threading.Thread(
                            target=handle_reject,
                            args=(sender_chat, dir_name, cb.get("message")),
                            daemon=True
                        ).start()
                    else:
                        requests.post(f"{BASE_URL}/answerCallbackQuery", json={"callback_query_id": cb["id"]})
                    continue

                # 2. Handle Text Messages
                msg = update.get("message", {})
                text = msg.get("text", "").strip()
                sender_chat = str(msg.get("chat", {}).get("id", ""))
                
                if not text:
                    continue
                if ALLOWED_CHAT and sender_chat != ALLOWED_CHAT:
                    continue
                
                cmd = text.lower()
                if cmd in ("/start", "/help"):
                    send_msg(sender_chat, (
                        "👋 *Welcome to MindSpark Studio Bot!*\n\n"
                        "*Commands:*\n"
                        "• Tap *PASS* button — Turant YouTube par video upload karein.\n"
                        "• Tap *REJECT* button — Video ko discard karein.\n"
                        "• Send `pass` or `/pass` — Latest video ko upload karein."
                    ))
                elif cmd in ("/pass", "pass", "upload", "y", "yes"):
                    threading.Thread(target=handle_pass_async, args=(sender_chat, None, msg), daemon=True).start()
                elif cmd in ("/reject", "reject", "no", "n"):
                    threading.Thread(target=handle_reject, args=(sender_chat, None, msg), daemon=True).start()
                elif cmd in ("/status", "status"):
                    target_dir = get_latest_output_dir()
                    if target_dir:
                        meta = jload(target_dir / "meta.json", {})
                        send_msg(sender_chat, f"📹 *Latest Local Video:* {meta.get('title', target_dir.name)}\n📁 Folder: `{target_dir.name}`\n👉 Tap PASS on the video to upload.")
                    else:
                        send_msg(sender_chat, "ℹ️ No local video found in output. Tap PASS on any video in chat to upload it to YouTube!")
        except Exception as e:
            time.sleep(2)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    start_bot()

FILE [83/92]: src/template_engine.py

"""Template Engine for MindSpark:
Selects from 22 distinct video templates with auto-rotation, weighted-random selection,
strict no-repeat history window, and full backward compatibility fallback.
"""
import os, sys, random, copy
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from common import ROOT, jload, jsave
import theme_engine

TEMPLATES_FILE = ROOT / "config" / "templates.json"
STYLE_FILE = ROOT / "config" / "style.json"
HISTORY_FILE = ROOT / "data" / "template_history.json"

DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = {
    "id": "parallax_dawn",
    "name": "Parallax Dawn",
    "description": "Warm mountain landscape, rising sun, cinematic depth and large punchy headlines.",
    "scene_recipe": ["hook", "story", "story", "story", "card"],
    "palette_family": ["Sunset Pop", "Gold Luxury", "Crimson Impact"],
    "font": "SpaceGrotesk",
    "layout": "center",
    "pattern": "rays",
    "transition": "slide",
    "caption_style": "pill",
    "backdrop": "landscape",
    "mascot": False,
    "weight": 10,
    "enabled": True
}

def load_templates():
    templates = jload(TEMPLATES_FILE, [])
    enabled = [t for t in templates if t.get("enabled", True)]
    return enabled if enabled else [DEFAULT_TEMPLATE]

def load_template_history():
    return jload(HISTORY_FILE, [])

def save_template_history(history):
    jsave(HISTORY_FILE, history)

def pick_template(history=None, config=None, rng=None):
    """Picks a template based on config/style.json rules (random, rotate, or fixed).
    Ensures no repetition within the last N videos (default 7).
    """
    try:
        all_templates = load_templates()
        if not all_templates:
            return copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE)
        
        cfg = config or jload(STYLE_FILE, {})
        hist = history if history is not None else load_template_history()
        mode = str(cfg.get("template_mode", "random")).lower()
        no_repeat_n = int(cfg.get("no_repeat_last", 7))
        
        if rng is None:
            seed = random.SystemRandom().randint(1, 10**9)
            rng = random.Random(seed)

        # 1. FIXED MODE
        if mode == "fixed":
            fixed_id = cfg.get("fixed_id", "parallax_dawn")
            chosen = next((t for t in all_templates if t["id"] == fixed_id), all_templates[0])
            return _enrich_template(chosen, hist, rng)

        # 2. ROTATE MODE
        if mode == "rotate":
            idx = len(hist) % len(all_templates)
            chosen = all_templates[idx]
            return _enrich_template(chosen, hist, rng)

        # 3. RANDOM MODE (with weighted pick and no-repeat window)
        recent_ids = [h.get("template_id") for h in hist[-no_repeat_n:] if isinstance(h, dict) and h.get("template_id")]
        
        eligible = [t for t in all_templates if t["id"] not in recent_ids]
        
        # If too strict and no eligible left, fallback to not repeating only the very last one
        if not eligible and recent_ids:
            last_id = recent_ids[-1]
            eligible = [t for t in all_templates if t["id"] != last_id]
        
        if not eligible:
            eligible = all_templates

        # Weighted selection
        weights = [max(1, int(t.get("weight", 10))) for t in eligible]
        chosen = rng.choices(eligible, weights=weights, k=1)[0]
        return _enrich_template(chosen, hist, rng)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ Template engine error: {e}. Falling back to default template.")
        return _enrich_template(copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE), history or [], rng or random.Random())

def _enrich_template(template, history, rng=None):
    """Enriches the template with dynamic palette, font, and strict 1080x1920 vertical format."""
    rng = rng or random.Random()
    t = copy.deepcopy(template)
    
    # Select palette from palette_family or fallback
    family = t.get("palette_family", [])
    available_palettes = [p for p in theme_engine.PALETTES if p["name"] in family]
    if not available_palettes:
        available_palettes = theme_engine.PALETTES

    recent_palettes = [h.get("palette") for h in history[-4:] if isinstance(h, dict) and h.get("palette")]
    fresh_palettes = [p for p in available_palettes if p["name"] not in recent_palettes]
    chosen_palette = rng.choice(fresh_palettes or available_palettes)

    # Allow slight dynamic variation in fonts/layouts if specified in theme_engine
    fonts = theme_engine.FONTS
    layouts = theme_engine.LAYOUTS
    patterns = theme_engine.PATTERNS
    transitions = theme_engine.TRANSITIONS

    chosen_font = t.get("font") or rng.choice(fonts)
    chosen_layout = t.get("layout") or rng.choice(layouts)
    chosen_pattern = t.get("pattern") or rng.choice(patterns)
    chosen_transition = t.get("transition") or rng.choice(transitions)

    # Strictly 9:16 vertical 1080x1920 format
    fmt = {
        "name": "vertical",
        "width": 1080,
        "height": 1920
    }

    t["theme"] = {
        "palette": chosen_palette["name"],
        "colors": {k: v for k, v in chosen_palette.items() if k != "name"},
        "font": chosen_font,
        "layout": chosen_layout,
        "pattern": chosen_pattern,
        "transition": chosen_transition,
        "chart": rng.choice(theme_engine.CHARTS),
        "formatName": "vertical",
        "format": fmt
    }
    t["template_id"] = t["id"]
    return t

def record_template_history(template, book_title=None):
    """Appends chosen template execution into data/template_history.json."""
    hist = load_template_history()
    import datetime
    entry = {
        "template_id": template.get("id"),
        "template_name": template.get("name"),
        "palette": template.get("theme", {}).get("palette"),
        "book": book_title or "",
        "date": str(datetime.date.today())
    }
    hist.append(entry)
    save_template_history(hist)
    return hist

"""Simulation test: verifies 30 consecutive daily runs with template engine
proving that no template repeats within the no_repeat_last window.
"""
import sys, os, random
from pathlib import Path

if sys.stdout and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
        sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import template_engine

def test_simulation(num_runs=30, window=7):
    print(f"=== Running Simulation: {num_runs} Days with no_repeat_last = {window} ===")
    history = []
    config = {"template_mode": "random", "no_repeat_last": window}
    rng = random.Random(42)  # reproducible seed
    
    violation_count = 0
    chosen_sequence = []

    for day in range(1, num_runs + 1):
        tmpl = template_engine.pick_template(history=history, config=config, rng=rng)
        tid = tmpl["id"]
        tname = tmpl["name"]
        pal = tmpl["theme"]["palette"]
        
        # Check window violation
        recent_window = [h["template_id"] for h in history[-window:]]
        if tid in recent_window:
            print(f"[FAIL] DAY {day:02d}: VIOLATION! '{tid}' repeated inside last {window} runs: {recent_window}")
            violation_count += 1
        else:
            print(f"[OK] DAY {day:02d}: [{tid:20s}] {tname:28s} | Palette: {pal:16s}")
            
        history.append({"template_id": tid, "palette": pal, "day": day})
        chosen_sequence.append(tid)

    unique_templates = len(set(chosen_sequence))
    print(f"\n--- Simulation Results ---")
    print(f"Total Runs: {num_runs}")
    print(f"Window Size: {window}")
    print(f"Unique Templates Used: {unique_templates}/22")
    print(f"Violations: {violation_count}")

    assert violation_count == 0, f"Expected 0 violations, got {violation_count}"
    print("\nALL NO-REPEAT CONSTRAINTS VERIFIED SUCCESSFULLY!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_simulation()

"""Picks a unique look for every video and avoids repeating recent looks. Strictly 9:16 Reel format."""
import os, random, time
from common import ROOT, jload

PALETTES = [
    {"name": "Neon Night", "bg1": "#0B1020", "bg2": "#1B1464", "ac": "#00F5A0", "tx": "#FFFFFF", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)"},
    {"name": "Crimson Impact", "bg1": "#120205", "bg2": "#2E080E", "ac": "#FF2A55", "tx": "#FFFFFF", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.12)"},
    {"name": "Sunset Pop", "bg1": "#FF5F6D", "bg2": "#FFC371", "ac": "#1B1B3A", "tx": "#1B1B3A", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.35)"},
    {"name": "Cyberpunk Cyan", "bg1": "#0D0221", "bg2": "#0F084B", "ac": "#00F0FF", "tx": "#FFFFFF", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.12)"},
    {"name": "Gold Luxury", "bg1": "#141414", "bg2": "#262117", "ac": "#FFD700", "tx": "#FDFDFD", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)"},
    {"name": "Ocean Deep", "bg1": "#0F4C75", "bg2": "#3282B8", "ac": "#FFD460", "tx": "#FFFFFF", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.12)"},
    {"name": "Mint Paper", "bg1": "#D4F5E9", "bg2": "#8FE3C4", "ac": "#E4572E", "tx": "#0B3D2E", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.50)"},
    {"name": "Violet Anime", "bg1": "#240046", "bg2": "#5A189A", "ac": "#FF9E00", "tx": "#FFFFFF", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.15)"},
    {"name": "Forest Emerald", "bg1": "#0B3D2E", "bg2": "#1F7A5A", "ac": "#F4D35E", "tx": "#F1FAEE", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.12)"},
    {"name": "Midnight Charcoal", "bg1": "#121417", "bg2": "#22272E", "ac": "#FF6B6B", "tx": "#F5F7FA", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)"},
    {"name": "Electric Cobalt", "bg1": "#03045E", "bg2": "#0077B6", "ac": "#90E0EF", "tx": "#FFFFFF", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.14)"},
    {"name": "Candy Pop", "bg1": "#FFB3C6", "bg2": "#FFE5EC", "ac": "#6A0572", "tx": "#3B0A45", "cd": "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)"},
]

FONTS = ["ArchivoBlack", "SpaceGrotesk", "Anton"]
LAYOUTS = ["center", "left"]
PATTERNS = ["grid", "dots", "rays", "diagonal"]
TRANSITIONS = ["wipe", "slide", "zoom", "flip"]
CHARTS = ["line", "area", "bars"]

# Strictly 9:16 Vertical Reel format (1080x1920)
FORMATS = {
    "vertical": (1080, 1920),
}

def _avoid(options, recent, rng):
    ok = [o for o in options if o not in recent]
    if not ok:
        last = recent[-1] if recent else None
        ok = [o for o in options if o != last] or list(options)
    return rng.choice(ok)

def pick_theme(book_title, allow_wide=False, history=None):
    history = jload(ROOT / "data" / "theme_history.json", []) if history is None else history
    # High entropy seed so every single video gets a fresh distinct theme
    seed = random.SystemRandom().randint(1, 10**9)
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    
    rec = lambda key, n: [h.get("theme", {}).get(key) for h in history[-n:] if isinstance(h.get("theme"), dict)]
    
    pal = _avoid([p["name"] for p in PALETTES], rec("palette", 8), rng)
    fmt = "vertical"  # 100% Reels / Shorts format
    
    return {
        "palette": pal,
        "colors": {k: v for k, v in next(p for p in PALETTES if p["name"] == pal).items() if k != "name"},
        "font": _avoid(FONTS, rec("font", 2), rng),
        "layout": _avoid(LAYOUTS, rec("layout", 2), rng),
        "pattern": _avoid(PATTERNS, rec("pattern", 2), rng),
        "transition": _avoid(TRANSITIONS, rec("transition", 2), rng),
        "chart": _avoid(CHARTS, rec("chart", 2), rng),
        "formatName": fmt,
        "format": {"name": fmt, "width": 1080, "height": 1920},
    }

"""Voice synthesis with Edge-TTS and ElevenLabs (Neil / custom) + word timings for captions."""
import os, json, base64, asyncio, subprocess, requests
from pathlib import Path
import edge_tts

def dur(p):
    return float(subprocess.check_output(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "csv=p=0", str(p)]))

def parse_elevenlabs_alignment(alignment):
    chars = alignment.get("characters", [])
    starts = alignment.get("character_start_times_seconds", [])
    ends = alignment.get("character_end_times_seconds", [])
    words = []
    curr_w, curr_start, curr_end = [], None, None
    for c, s, e in zip(chars, starts, ends):
        if c.isspace():
            if curr_w:
                words.append({"w": "".join(curr_w), "s": round(curr_start, 3), "e": round(curr_end, 3)})
                curr_w, curr_start = [], None
        else:
            if curr_start is None:
                curr_start = s
            curr_end = e
            curr_w.append(c)
    if curr_w:
        words.append({"w": "".join(curr_w), "s": round(curr_start, 3), "e": round(curr_end, 3)})
    return words

def _synth_elevenlabs(text, voice_id, api_key, out):
    url = f"https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/text-to-speech/{voice_id}/with-timestamps"
    headers = {"xi-api-key": api_key, "Content-Type": "application/json"}
    payload = {
        "text": text,
        "model_id": "eleven_multilingual_v2",
        "voice_settings": {"stability": 0.5, "similarity_boost": 0.8}
    }
    resp = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=60)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    data = resp.json()
    with open(out, "wb") as f:
        f.write(base64.b64decode(data["audio_base64"]))
    return parse_elevenlabs_alignment(data.get("alignment", {}))

async def _synth_edge(text, cfg, out):
    voice = cfg.get("voice_model") or cfg.get("voice") or "en-IN-PrabhatNeural"
    if not voice.startswith(("en-", "hi-", "Microsoft")):
        voice = "en-IN-PrabhatNeural"
    kw = dict(rate=cfg.get("rate", "+0%"), pitch=cfg.get("pitch", "+0Hz"))
    try:
        comm = edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice, boundary="WordBoundary", **kw)
    except TypeError:
        comm = edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice, **kw)
    words = []
    with open(out, "wb") as f:
        async for ch in comm.stream():
            if ch["type"] == "audio":
                f.write(ch["data"])
            elif ch["type"] == "WordBoundary":
                words.append({"w": ch["text"], "s": round(ch["offset"] / 1e7, 3), "e": round((ch["offset"] + ch["duration"]) / 1e7, 3)})
    return words

def synth_speech(text, cfg, out):
    provider = cfg.get("provider", "").lower()
    el_key = os.getenv("ELEVENLABS_API_KEY") or os.getenv("XI_API_KEY") or cfg.get("elevenlabs_api_key")
    if (provider == "elevenlabs" or cfg.get("voice") == "elevenlabs_neil") and el_key:
        voice_id = cfg.get("elevenlabs_voice_id") or "AZnzlk1XvdvUeBnXmlld" # Neil default ID
        try:
            return _synth_elevenlabs(text, voice_id, el_key, out)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[TTS] ElevenLabs call failed ({e}), falling back to Edge-TTS neural.")
    return asyncio.run(_synth_edge(text, cfg, out))

def narrate(scenes, workdir, cfg, pad=0.45):
    workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    parts = []
    for i, s in enumerate(scenes):
        raw, padded = workdir / f"s{i}_raw.mp3", workdir / f"s{i}.mp3"
        words = synth_speech(s["narration"], cfg, raw)
        subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", raw, "-af", f"apad=pad_dur={pad}", "-ar", "44100", "-ac", "2", padded], check=True)
        s["durationSec"] = round(dur(padded), 3)
        if not words:  # fallback: evenly spaced words
            toks = s["narration"].split()
            step = (s["durationSec"] - pad) / max(1, len(toks))
            words = [{"w": t, "s": round(k * step, 3), "e": round((k + 1) * step, 3)} for k, t in enumerate(toks)]
        s["words"] = words
        parts.append(padded)
    lst = workdir / "list.txt"
    lst.write_text("".join(f"file '{p.resolve().as_posix()}'\n" for p in parts))
    out = workdir / "voice.mp3"
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", lst, "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-q:a", "3", out], check=True)
    return out

"""Verification script: renders and tests all 22 templates with Remotion.
Checks ffprobe dimensions (1080x1920), records render time, and generates verification table.
"""
import os, sys, time, json, shutil, subprocess
from pathlib import Path

if sys.stdout and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
        sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if str(ROOT / "src") not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import template_engine

def get_dimensions(file_path):
    try:
        cmd = [
            "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0",
            "-show_entries", "stream=width,height",
            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0", str(file_path)
        ]
        out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).decode().strip()
        return out
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

def verify_all():
    templates = template_engine.load_templates()
    print(f"=== Verifying All {len(templates)} Templates with Remotion ===")
    
    npx = shutil.which("npx.cmd") or shutil.which("npx") or "npx.cmd"
    test_out_dir = ROOT / "data" / "test_renders"
    test_out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    results = []

    for idx, t in enumerate(templates, 1):
        tid = t["id"]
        tname = t["name"]
        plan_file = ROOT / "data" / f"demo_plan_{tid}.json"
        out_image = test_out_dir / f"test_{tid}.png"

        if not plan_file.exists():
            results.append((tid, tname, "FAIL", "0.0s", "Plan not found"))
            continue

        print(f"[{idx:02d}/22] Testing '{tid}' ({tname})...", end=" ", flush=True)
        t0 = time.time()
        
        # Test full Remotion render pipeline
        cmd = [
            npx, "remotion", "still", "src/index.ts", "Main",
            str(out_image),
            f"--props={plan_file.resolve().as_posix()}",
            "--frame=25",
            "--log=error"
        ]
        
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(ROOT / "remotion"), capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
            elapsed = time.time() - t0
            dims = get_dimensions(out_image)
            status = "OK" if dims == "1080x1920" else "FAIL"
            results.append((tid, tname, status, f"{elapsed:.1f}s", dims))
            print(f"OK ({elapsed:.1f}s, {dims})")
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as err:
            elapsed = time.time() - t0
            err_msg = (err.stderr or err.stdout or "Render error")[:80].strip()
            print(f"FAIL: {err_msg}")
            results.append((tid, tname, "FAIL", f"{elapsed:.1f}s", "Render Error"))

    print("\n" + "=" * 85)
    print("### TEMPLATE VERIFICATION RESULTS")
    print("=" * 85)
    print("| # | Template ID | Template Name | Render Status | Render Time | Resolution |")
    print("|---|-------------|---------------|---------------|-------------|------------|")
    for i, r in enumerate(results, 1):
        print(f"| {i:02d} | `{r[0]}` | {r[1]} | **{r[2]}** | {r[3]} | `{r[4]}` |")
    print("=" * 85)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_all()

from .youtube_client import YouTubeUploader, youtube_uploader

================================================================================
FILE [89/92]: src/youtube/youtube_client.py
================================================================================
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from pathlib import Path
from config.settings import config

logger = logging.getLogger("YouTubeUploader")

class YouTubeUploader:
    def __init__(self):
        self.client_secrets_file = config.youtube.client_secrets_file
        self.token_file = config.youtube.token_file
        self.scopes = config.youtube.scopes

    def is_configured(self) -> bool:
        """
        Returns True if client_secrets.json exists.
        """
        return os.path.exists(self.client_secrets_file)

    def is_authorized(self) -> bool:
        """
        Returns True if an authorized user token exists and is valid.
        """
        if not os.path.exists(self.token_file):
            return False
        try:
            from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials
            from google.auth.transport.requests import Request
            creds = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(self.token_file, self.scopes)
            if creds and creds.valid:
                return True
            if creds and creds.expired and creds.refresh_token:
                creds.refresh(Request())
                with open(self.token_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(creds.to_json())
                return True
            return False
        except Exception:
            return False

    def get_auth_url(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Generates OAuth 2.0 authorization URL for the user to authorize their channel.
        """
        if not self.is_configured():
            return None
        try:
            from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import Flow
            flow = Flow.from_client_secrets_file(
                self.client_secrets_file,
                scopes=self.scopes,
                redirect_uri="urn:ietf:wg:oauth:2.0:oob"
            )
            auth_url, _ = flow.authorization_url(prompt="consent", access_type="offline")
            return auth_url
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating auth URL: {e}")
            return None

    def finish_auth_with_code(self, code: str) -> bool:
        """
        Exchanges the authorization code for access and refresh tokens and stores them securely.
        """
        if not self.is_configured():
            return False
        try:
            from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import Flow
            flow = Flow.from_client_secrets_file(
                self.client_secrets_file,
                scopes=self.scopes,
                redirect_uri="urn:ietf:wg:oauth:2.0:oob"
            )
            flow.fetch_token(code=code.strip())
            creds = flow.credentials

            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.token_file), exist_ok=True)
            with open(self.token_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(creds.to_json())
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to exchange code: {e}")
            return False

    def upload_video(
        self,
        video_path: str,
        title: str,
        description: str,
        tags: Optional[list] = None,
        privacy_status: str = "private",
        thumbnail_path: Optional[str] = None,
        dry_run: bool = False,
        progress_callback: Optional[Any] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Uploads an MP4 video to YouTube Data API v3.
        Defaults strictly to 'private'.
        """
        if dry_run:
            return {
                "status": "dry_run_success",
                "video_id": "DRY_RUN_MOCK_ID",
                "video_url": "https://youtu.be/DRY_RUN_MOCK_ID",
                "privacy": privacy_status,
                "message": "Dry run upload simulated successfully without hitting API quota."
            }

        if not self.is_authorized():
            return {
                "status": "not_authorized",
                "error": "YouTube OAuth credentials not found or expired. Please authorize channel in Settings."
            }

        try:
            from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials
            from google.auth.transport.requests import Request
            from googleapiclient.discovery import build
            from googleapiclient.http import MediaFileUpload

            creds = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(self.token_file, self.scopes)
            if creds and creds.expired and creds.refresh_token:
                creds.refresh(Request())
                with open(self.token_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(creds.to_json())

            youtube = build("youtube", "v3", credentials=creds)

            body = {
                "snippet": {
                    "title": title[:100],
                    "description": description[:5000],
                    "tags": tags or ["Hindi explainer", "MindSpark"],
                    "categoryId": "27",  # Education category
                    "defaultLanguage": "hi",
                    "defaultAudioLanguage": "hi"
                },
                "status": {
                    "privacyStatus": privacy_status if privacy_status in ["private", "unlisted", "public"] else "private",
                    "selfDeclaredMadeForKids": False
                }
            }

            media = MediaFileUpload(
                video_path,
                mimetype="video/mp4",
                resumable=True,
                chunksize=1024 * 1024 * 1  # 1MB chunks for smooth live progress
            )

            request = youtube.videos().insert(
                part="snippet,status",
                body=body,
                media_body=media
            )

            response = None
            while response is None:
                status, response = request.next_chunk()
                if status:
                    pct = int(status.progress() * 100)
                    logger.info(f"Uploaded {pct}%")
                    if progress_callback:
                        try:
                            progress_callback(pct)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

            video_id = response.get("id")
            video_url = f"https://youtu.be/{video_id}"

            # Try uploading custom thumbnail if provided
            if thumbnail_path and os.path.exists(thumbnail_path):
                try:
                    youtube.thumbnails().set(
                        videoId=video_id,
                        media_body=MediaFileUpload(thumbnail_path, mimetype="image/jpeg")
                    ).execute()
                    logger.info(f"Custom thumbnail uploaded for video {video_id}")
                except Exception as te:
                    logger.warning(f"Note: Custom thumbnail upload skipped or requires channel verification: {te}")

            return {
                "status": "success",
                "video_id": video_id,
                "video_url": video_url,
                "privacy": privacy_status
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Upload failed: {e}")
            return {
                "status": "error",
                "error": str(e)
            }

youtube_uploader = YouTubeUploader()

"""PASS = upload to YouTube, save book in completed_books.md, delete leftovers."""
import os, sys, shutil, pathlib, datetime
from common import ROOT, load_env, jload

def append_completed_book(title, author="", category="Self Improvement", video_url=""):
    cp = ROOT / "completed_books.md"
    content = cp.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if cp.exists() else ""
    lines = [line for line in content.splitlines() if line.strip().startswith("|") and not line.strip().startswith("| No.") and not line.strip().startswith("| :---")]
    next_no = len(lines) + 1
    today_str = datetime.date.today().isoformat()
    new_row = f"| {next_no} | {today_str} | {title} | {author or 'Unknown'} | {category} | {video_url or 'Archived'} |\n"
    with open(cp, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(new_row)

def upload(d, dry_run=False, progress_callback=None):
    d = pathlib.Path(d)
    meta = jload(d / "meta.json", {})
    video_path = str(d / "final.mp4")
    title = meta.get("title", f"{d.name} Summary #MindSpark")[:100]
    description = meta.get("description", "")
    tags = meta.get("tags", ["Shorts", "MindSpark", "Books"])
    privacy = meta.get("privacy", "private")

    # If dry_run is set via flag or env var
    if dry_run or os.environ.get("DRY_RUN", "").lower() in ("1", "true"):
        print(f"[DRY RUN] Simulating upload for '{title}' (privacy: {privacy})")
        if progress_callback:
            progress_callback(100)
        return "dry_run_video_id"

    # Try local OAuth client first (from old project)
    token_file = ROOT / "config" / "youtube_token.json"
    secrets_file = ROOT / "config" / "client_secrets.json"
    if token_file.exists() and secrets_file.exists():
        try:
            sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
            from src.youtube.youtube_client import youtube_uploader
            res = youtube_uploader.upload_video(
                video_path=video_path,
                title=title,
                description=description,
                tags=tags,
                privacy_status=privacy,
                dry_run=False,
                progress_callback=progress_callback
            )
            return res.get("video_id", "uploaded")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Warning: Local OAuth upload failed ({e}), trying environment credentials...")

    # Fallback to CI / GitHub Actions environment variables
    from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials
    from googleapiclient.discovery import build
    from googleapiclient.http import MediaFileUpload
    creds = Credentials(None, refresh_token=os.environ["YT_REFRESH_TOKEN"], token_uri="https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token",
                        client_id=os.environ["YT_CLIENT_ID"], client_secret=os.environ["YT_CLIENT_SECRET"],
                        scopes=["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload"])
    yt = build("youtube", "v3", credentials=creds)
    body = {"snippet": {"title": title, "description": description, "tags": tags, "categoryId": "27"},
            "status": {"privacyStatus": privacy, "selfDeclaredMadeForKids": False}}
    req = yt.videos().insert(part="snippet,status", body=body, media_body=MediaFileUpload(video_path, chunksize=1024*1024, resumable=True))
    resp = None
    while resp is None:
        status, resp = req.next_chunk()
        if status and progress_callback:
            try:
                progress_callback(int(status.progress() * 100))
            except Exception:
                pass
    return resp["id"]

def approve(d, dry_run=False):
    d = pathlib.Path(d)
    meta = jload(d / "meta.json", {})
    vid = upload(d, dry_run=dry_run)
    book_title = meta.get("book", d.name)
    video_url = f"https://youtu.be/{vid}" if vid and vid != "dry_run_video_id" else "Archived"
    append_completed_book(book_title, meta.get("author", ""), "Productivity & Wisdom", video_url)
    shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)
    return vid

if __name__ == "__main__":
    load_env()
    for p in sys.argv[1:]:
        print(p, "->", approve(p))

import os
import pytest
from pathlib import Path
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "src"))

from src.daily_pipeline import pick_book
from src.llm import TYPES, NEED, validate
from src.theme_engine import pick_theme
import src.template_engine as template_engine

def test_pick_book_selection():
    forced = pick_book([], forced="Atomic Habits")
    assert forced["title"] == "Atomic Habits"

    book = pick_book([])
    assert "title" in book
    assert "author" in book

def test_all_22_templates_loaded():
    templates = template_engine.load_templates()
    assert len(templates) == 22
    for t in templates:
        assert "id" in t
        assert "name" in t
        assert "scene_recipe" in t
        assert "palette_family" in t
        assert "weight" in t
        assert t.get("enabled", True) is True

def test_template_engine_modes():
    # Fixed mode
    t_fixed = template_engine.pick_template(config={"template_mode": "fixed", "fixed_id": "retro_terminal"})
    assert t_fixed["id"] == "retro_terminal"
    assert t_fixed["theme"]["format"]["width"] == 1080
    assert t_fixed["theme"]["format"]["height"] == 1920

    # Rotate mode
    t_rot0 = template_engine.pick_template(history=[], config={"template_mode": "rotate"})
    t_rot1 = template_engine.pick_template(history=[{"template_id": t_rot0["id"]}], config={"template_mode": "rotate"})
    assert t_rot0["id"] != t_rot1["id"]

    # Random mode with no_repeat_last
    hist = [{"template_id": "parallax_dawn"}, {"template_id": "neon_city_night"}]
    t_rand = template_engine.pick_template(history=hist, config={"template_mode": "random", "no_repeat_last": 2})
    assert t_rand["id"] not in ("parallax_dawn", "neon_city_night")

def test_new_scene_types_in_llm_registry():
    new_types = [
        "quiz", "kineticBurst", "paper", "terminal", "stickyNotes", "comicPanels",
        "chalkboard", "timeline", "countdown", "beforeAfter", "quotePoster",
        "habitTracker", "cyberGlitch", "paperCut", "gradientFlow"
    ]
    for nt in new_types:
        assert nt in TYPES, f"Missing {nt} in TYPES"
        assert nt in NEED, f"Missing {nt} in NEED"

def test_template_recipe_plan_validation():
    # Test valid plan matching spark_quiz recipe
    t_quiz = next(t for t in template_engine.load_templates() if t["id"] == "spark_quiz")
    quiz_plan = {
        "youtube": {"title": "Pop Quiz On Atomic Habits"},
        "scenes": [
            {"type": "hook", "narration": "Yeh video dekho.", "data": {"title": "WHICH HABIT WINS"}},
            {"type": "quiz", "narration": "Option A ya Option B?", "data": {"question": "What brings 37x growth?", "options": ["A", "B", "C"]}},
            {"type": "card", "narration": "System beats goals.", "data": {"lines": ["Make cue obvious", "Make craving attractive"]}},
            {"type": "quote", "narration": "Final wisdom quote.", "data": {"quote": "Every action is a vote for who you want to be."}}
        ]
    }
    err = validate(quiz_plan, template=t_quiz)
    assert err is None

def test_theme_engine_selection():
    theme = pick_theme("Deep Work", False, [])
    assert "palette" in theme
    assert "colors" in theme
    assert "bg1" in theme["colors"]
    assert "tx" in theme["colors"]
    assert "ac" in theme["colors"]
    assert "transition" in theme
    assert theme["format"]["width"] == 1080
    assert theme["format"]["height"] == 1920

import pytest, sys, pathlib
sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
from src.youtube.youtube_client import youtube_uploader

def test_youtube_uploader_configured():
    assert youtube_uploader.is_configured() is True

def test_youtube_uploader_authorized():
    assert youtube_uploader.is_authorized() is True

def test_youtube_uploader_dry_run():
    res = youtube_uploader.upload_video(
        video_path="dummy.mp4",
        title="Test YouTube Upload",
        description="Testing clean YouTube upload pipeline",
        tags=["Test", "MindSpark"],
        privacy_status="private",
        dry_run=True
    )
    assert res["status"] == "dry_run_success"
    assert res["video_id"] == "DRY_RUN_MOCK_ID"
    assert "https://youtu.be/DRY_RUN_MOCK_ID" in res["video_url"]
    assert res["privacy"] == "private"
